(3) **Select** (`pick-shorts.tsx`): tick boxes over the rows, Shift for a range, a day's box, Ctrl/Cmd+A, Esc; a bar at the foot (Export: a zip, a file each, `lib/zip.ts` stored with UTF-8 names; Move to: masters, asked first). (4) **Deleting**: `lib/remove-animation.ts` `rowAway` (a red line through the title's words, the row slides off, the gap closes; the server is asked after the slide; it comes back if deleting fails) and `pageAway` on a short's page (title struck, the page falls away, then the list); animations off: a fade; the new `remove` sound. (5) `safeFileName` (lib/image/compress.ts) drops accents (ș → s) instead of turning them into dashes. Checked: `tests/short-bulk.test.ts` (new; 30 planted mistakes caught), `tests/long-lists.test.ts` (reads the table where it is now); in the harness (`scenes/shorts-table.tsx` with `t-shorts-table.mjs`, 52 checks: the PDF opened with poppler and qpdf, text, lists, link and picture in it, the Word file unzipped, a short with no script, Move to step and Undo, the delete's line, slide and timing, a failed delete coming back, picking with Shift, a day, all, Space and Esc, a zip of PDFs and of Word files checked with unzip, moving many with one that can't, Undo, someone who isn't a master, the phone bar, a short's page falling away).
- **1.37.0** (Oct 27 2026): **Media kit.** (0114; no new dependency) The owner's answers of Oct 27 2026 (09:59): "show rates or say on request … one kit per channel / per team with all the channels … be able to set a lot of things before generating it. The design has to be wonderful and beautiful". Analytics → **Media kit** (`?tab=mediakit`, the last tab, locked like Revenue): a 16:9 PDF deck made in the browser with pdf-lib (like Download for Claude) and previewed page by page as SVG from the same list of drawing steps, so the preview is the PDF. (1) **Setup** (`modules/media-kit/lib/settings.ts`; a Master's is saved 0.9 s after a change, `saveMediaKit`): the channels; one kit with everything or one per platform; the period (28 days by default, 90 days, 365 days or custom dates; another period's numbers come from `loadMediaKitNumbers`); the pages (Cover, About us, At a glance, a page per platform, Audience, Best videos and how many, Brands and kind words, Rates, Contact); light or dark; the team's colour or any other; the cover (the best thumbnails, an uploaded photo, or a big number); title, tagline, about, a note on the audience; rates with prices (packages: name, what's in it, platform, price; the currency; a note) or "on request"; brands and up to three quotes; a contact name, email, phone, website and the closing line. A stored cover photo's link is signed again (`mediaKitCoverLink`) after 45 minutes and before a download. (2) **Numbers** (`gather.ts`, server only, `kitTeam` = `canViewRevenue` first): the Audience tab's followers and views (with the period before), engagement, watch hours, posts, average views (long and short), day by day, the most viewed videos with their thumbnails (`/api/analytics/thumb`), ages, genders and countries where the platform gives them; the all-time best when the period has none. (3) **Pages** (`layout.ts`, `scene.ts`, `theme.ts`): measured with the PDF's own fonts (Inter whole, Fraunces subset; the loaders moved to `lib/pdf-kit.ts`, shared with the Claude export), colours worked out from the accent (OKLCH; every text checked for contrast in both themes and eight accents), links in the PDF (videos, profiles, email, website), and a closing note on where the numbers come from and when they were read. (4) **Access**: Masters and revenue people see and download; Masters save and upload the photo; deleting a team removes its `media-kit` files. Checked: the migration from scratch with every database test (`media-kit.test.sql`); `tests/media-kit.test.ts` (new); in the sandbox's harness (`scenes/media-kit-pages.tsx` with `t-mediakit.mjs`: every page light and dark, eight accents, one platform, no numbers, long texts, the real PDF rasterised with poppler next to the preview; `scenes/media-kit-builder.tsx` with `t-mediakit-builder.mjs`: 26 checks of the builder and its download).
- **1.36.1** (Oct 27 2026): **Filming step, ready to go live.** (no migration; no new dependency) CI's type check failed on 1.36.0: `getShortSettings` builds its select at run time (the default filmer only once 0113 ran), and supabase-js types a select that isn't a string literal as `GenericStringError`, so reading `data.shorts_per_day` and the rest didn't compile. The row is now read as plain fields (`as Record<string, unknown>`, as `createShort` already did). The sandbox's type check missed it because its Supabase packages were stand-ins; it now uses the real supabase-js 2.118.0 and @supabase/ssr 0.5.2 types (see Working notes) and reproduces CI's ten errors on 1.36.0, none on 1.36.1.
- **1.36.0** (Oct 26 2026): **A Filming step for shorts.** (0112, 0113; no new dependency) The owner's list of Oct 26 2026, 17:36 (part 3 of 3): "for shorts, i want a filming step, the same as longs". Script → **Filming** → Editing (`SHORT_STAGES`, blue, "Shorts in Filming" in search). (1) **Sending it**: Script's button is now **Send to filming** (the master; `sendShortToFilming`, `canSendToFilming`; `sendShortToEditing` / `canSendToEditing` are gone): its filmer is notified (`short_filming`), or every filmer when nobody is set; no editor is needed yet. (2) **Filming card** (`app/(dashboard)/shorts/[id]/filming-card.tsx`, the long video Film step's TurnBox): its filmer, any filmer while nobody is set, or a master types the NAS folder (optional) and clicks "Filmed and uploaded to the NAS" (after a yes) → `markShortFilmed` → `short_mark_filmed`: it moves to Editing and its editor hears it's theirs (`short_filmed`, `toEditor`); with no editor yet, the masters, told it needs one. Afterwards the card says who filmed it and when, with the folder to copy and "Change the folder" for its filmer, its editor, a scheduler or a master (`setShortFootagePath`, `canEditFootage`); at Editing the folder is above the video for the editor (`FootageFolder`). At Filming the page shows the script (to film from) and no video card. (3) **Who films it**: a Filmer picker in the short's settings (People: Filmer, Editor, Reviewer, Scheduler), in New short, and the team's default filmer (Team → Defaults → Short videos; `updateShortSettings`); "Any filmer" when nobody is picked; a "Filmer" chip in the short's header; made the filmer at Filming: "It's ready to film." The history says "sent it to filming" and "filmed it". (4) **Everywhere the steps are listed**: My tasks "Film it" at Filming (five steps), the Pipeline widget, Analytics' time in each step, the master's Move to (refused before the migrations). Filming a short: 30 XP, counted as filming (the filming achievements say "videos" now; `TASK_XP` "Scripting or filming a short"). "Assigned to me" on the shorts list counts the shorts you film. Search's step pills use their word shade (the new blue read about 2.5:1 on the dark palette). (5) **Deploy window**: until 0113 runs, `hasShortFilming()` (one probe per request) leaves the new columns out of every read and write; Send to filming and Move to Filming say to run 0112 and 0113. Checked: the migrations from scratch on Postgres 16 with every database test (`short-filming.test.sql`: only the master sends it to filming; marked filmed by its filmer, not by another filmer or someone without the role, not by moving the step or writing when by hand, not twice; with nobody set, any filmer, the folder it had kept; who may change the folder and who films it (someone on the team), the default filmer on the team; the filmer's task waiting, active, done, then the editor's; 30 XP counted as filming; 0111 kept; each part proven to catch a planted mistake); in the harness (`scenes/short-filming.tsx` with `t-filming.mjs`: the step strip, Send to filming and its question, Filmed with the folder, Change the folder, Copy, someone else's turn, light, dark and phone width). Tests: `tests/short-filming.test.ts` (new; 41 planted mistakes caught), `tests/light-contrast.test.ts`.
- **1.35.2** (Oct 26 2026): **Scripts sent once, tasks done early.** (0111; no new dependency) The owner's list of Oct 26 2026, 17:36 (part 2 of 3): "in scripting, once you get the script to staging or review, you can still click on the send button, which is bad, we should grey it out until the next step is done" and "for tasks, in upcoming for example, if you do the tasks earlier than the date they are set for, will they automatically be done once the day comes? i feel like we should count them as soon as you do them even if they're upcoming and just have in our task history written: \"done early on date\"". (1) **Script hand-offs** (`script-flow.tsx` `HandOffButton`): once a step is sent on, its button ("Sent to review · 2h ago") is greyed out and does nothing, its tooltip saying when it opens again, until the next step is done since: the Script's until Review has sent it on to Staging or the script is marked done; Review's until "Staging done" (taking that back greys it again). Then it's the green "Sent to … · ago" that sends it again (a new round). `handOffLocked` (`modules/scripts/lib/flow-lock.ts`, pure, `LOCK_WAIT`) for the strip (with the done shown at once after a click) and the server: `handOffScript` asks `handOffWait` first (the video's flow documents and their `script_handoffs`, newest of each) and answers "Already sent to review. You can send it again once the review is done." (2) **Tasks count when they're done**: every step's task already was done the moment its video moved past the step, whatever its date; the one exception was a short's Ready to post task ("Schedule the posts"), which stayed open until the short was Posted, its last post out on the day, however early it was all scheduled. Migration **0111**: done as soon as every platform the short goes to has its post set up (scheduled, on its way or out, or marked posted by hand), and the same task back if one is then cancelled or fails while the short is still at Ready to post (see the migration list). XP and achievements follow (they read the done tasks; done before its date it counts as on time, two days before as early). (3) **"Done early" in the history**: `listDone` brings each task's `due_date` (`Done.due`); My tasks → Done and a day on Contributions add "Done early on Oct 26, due Oct 29" under a task finished before its due day, the day where you are (`doneEarly`, `earlyText`, `modules/dashboard/lib/done-early.ts`). Checked: the migrations from scratch on Postgres 16 with every database test (`ready-task-scheduled.test.sql`: one platform of two scheduled stays open; both: done, the same task, its date kept; a re-sync adds nothing; cancelled: the same task back with the new date; scheduled again, failed, retried; a post's progress changes nothing; posted later: still done when it was scheduled; a platform taken off or added, one marked posted by hand; a short not yet at Ready to post waits and follows its date, and is done as it gets there with everything scheduled; people still read only their own tasks and write none; each part proven to catch a planted mistake); in the harness (`scenes/script-flow.tsx`: the strip in each state, light and dark, the greyed button does nothing, the green one opens Send again; `scenes/tasks-done.tsx`: the Done tab). Tests: `tests/script-handoff-lock.test.ts`, `tests/done-early.test.ts` (new; each guard proven to catch a planted mistake).
- **1.35.1** (Oct 26 2026): **Dashboard filters, and a smoother wallpaper.** (no migration; no new dependency) The owner's list of Oct 26 2026, 17:36 (part 1 of 3: then 1.35.2 scripts and early tasks, 1.36.0 a Filming step for shorts), plus 20:45: "for the upcoming shorts and longs widgets, i want the filters to look the same as the followers and views, i also want to be able to filter by editor and filter by only instagram or only 1 platform in particular. add sounds to the filters just like the other widgets … same for longs too, but its only youtube basically, and i want the thumbnails to show up in upcoming long", "turning wallpaper off should also give you the loading screen thing", "selecting a wallpaper should too" and "top videos widget i want to be able to scroll inside it so theres like the top 20 videos and a button to see view all on the top right too". (1) **One kind of filter chip** (`modules/dashboard/components/filter-chips.tsx` `FilterChips` / `chipClass`): Followers' and Views' look (a radio group, the picked chip ink on paper, numbers when the widget is wide, arrow keys, Tab on the picked one or the first); `PlatformChips` (Views, Followers) and Top videos' chips use it. A click ticks through SoundSync (any role="radio" or role="checkbox"), so the filters sound like the other widgets' switches; Upcoming shorts' old `PlatformFilter` (aria-pressed) made no sound. (2) **Upcoming shorts** (`team-widgets.tsx`): the platform chips (each with how many you see when you pick it, with the editor filter as it is), an editor row under them (each editor's face, their first name when the widget is 420 px wide, and "No editor yet" when some have nobody; shown when there's a choice), and **Only** (a checkbox chip next to a picked platform, shown when some of its shorts go elsewhere too, or it's on): the shorts that go to that platform and nowhere else. All three are saved (`settings.platform`, `.only`, `.editor` = the editor's team membership id; `CATALOG.upcomingShorts.settings`), through `useSaved`; "Show all shorts" on an empty result resets both filters in ONE `onSettings` call (`updateSettings` starts from the layout as rendered, so two calls in a row lose the first). Narrow widgets (under 165 px) get tighter chips; under 90 px tall the chips hide and the gear has the same choices (Platform, Only that platform, Editor). The pure part: `modules/dashboard/lib/upcoming-filters.ts` (`goesTo`, `editedBy`, `editorsOf`: busiest first, `editorPick`: a saved editor who isn't on any of these any more means every editor, `editorChoice`, `nothingFor`: "No upcoming shorts edited by Dan that go only to TikTok."). Faces' initials use `onFill`. (3) **Upcoming longs**: the same editor chips (`settings.editor`, `CATALOG.upcomingLongs.settings`, `HAS_SETTINGS`, `renderWidget`), each video's editors (its Edit step's `project_assignees`, `listUpcoming`), its thumbnail 68 px wide in a widget of 340 px or more (52, 44 narrower), and in a narrow widget the date after the step ("Edit · Wed", `dueWord`/`dueTitle` from `tasks-widget.tsx`) so the title has room. **Thumbnails**: `itemsFor` (queries.ts) took only Thumbnail Studio's winners, so most videos in progress showed the plain long-video mark; now the winner, else the first Ideate sketch (`project_thumbnails`, `sketchThumb(…, 160)`), as the long videos list does (also for My tasks' long-video rows). The loading bones are rows now (they were two big cards). (4) **Top videos**: the 20 best (`TOP_SHOWN`; was as many rows as fit, at most 12) in a list that scrolls inside the widget (`widget-scroll`); the server sends each platform's 20 best of the 200 most viewed (`TOP_EACH`, `loadDashAudience`; was 8 of 60). **View all** at the card's top right (`GridCardMeta.link`, rendered by `GridCard` beside the title, not while customizing; `widgetMeta` sets it for Top videos with `topVideosHref(settings)`, layout.ts): Analytics → Content, 28 days, `&platform=` the one the widget shows, which `ContentTab` now opens on (`useSearchParams`). (5) **The wallpaper under Clip's screen**: `changeLook` takes an `apply` that returns a promise (`LookRequest`), and `LookScreenHost` stays up until it settles (`runChanges` in lib/look-screen.ts: changes in order, the slow ones awaited, a failure or a throw skipped, at most `LOOK_MAX_WAIT` 8 s); new words "Putting up your wallpaper" / "Taking your wallpaper down". `dashboard-background.tsx` `underScreen(what, change)`: the server action, then `refreshed()` (a `router.refresh()` in the component's transition, settled when the transition is done, at most 6 s), then `wallpaperDrawn()` (DashboardBackdrop's new layer has loaded, at most 3 s): On/Off, picking one, a new upload going up, On every page (when it's showing), removing the one showing; the darkness slider and making one lighter stay as they were. Checked in the harness with Chromium: `scenes/widgets.tsx` (Upcoming shorts and longs at 180 to 520 px, filters saved, dark), `scenes/studio.tsx` (new: the real DashboardStudio and GridBoard with sample data; `@dnd-kit` stubbed in the harness) with `t-studio.mjs` (Top videos 20, scrolling, a wheel, View all at the top right and following the pick, opening Content on Instagram; Upcoming shorts' platform, Only (#233 and #237: Instagram alone), editor, the empty text and Show all with one save, arrow keys; Upcoming longs' editors; each click ticks: counted oscillators), `scenes/wallpaper.tsx` (new: Settings' Wallpaper with the look screen, a stand-in server and a refresh that suspends like Next's) with `t-wallpaper.mjs` (timed from inside the page: for Off, picking, On every page off and on, removing the one showing, the screen is up before the save, starts leaving only after the page has the new state and its picture, and is gone 1.5 to 2 s after the click; light, dark, with screenshots), and `t-contrast.mjs` on the widgets and the board in six looks (nothing under 4.5:1 but rows scrolled out of view). Tests: `tests/dashboard-upcoming.test.ts` (new), `tests/look-screen.test.ts` (the slow changes and the wallpaper), `tests/dashboard-platforms.test.ts`, `tests/dashboard-graphs.test.ts`, `tests/pictures-size.test.ts` updated (each guard proven to catch a planted mistake).
- **1.35.0** (Oct 26 2026): **Light mode, easy to read.** (no migration; no new dependency) The owner's list of Oct 25 2026, 22:27 (part 5 of 5): "light mode needs a complete rework across the app, i want big focus on this so that light mode also looks good, take an entire version for this and check contrast and things everywhere with every possible combination". Measured first (harness `t-contrast.mjs`: every piece of text on screen against the pixels under it, with the text made invisible; exempt: disabled, hidden, covered): in light mode the accent's words were 2.7–3.3:1 on its own chips, white on the Default / Ink orange buttons 3.4:1, green, short, long, gold and coral words 2.2–3.0, ink-faint 4.1 on Nord's paper, words on the glass wash down to 3.4 (a dark theme accent made a grey wash), avatars' initials 2.9–3.7; dark mode had blue, pink, red and teal words at 2.4–3.1. (1) **Word shades** (`globals.css`): every colour has its fill (`--amber`, `--teal`…: bg, border, ring, fill, stroke, unchanged in use) and its word shade (`--amber-text`…), which `text-amber` and the rest now use (`tailwind.config.ts` `textColor`): the same hue with its OKLCH lightness moved (darker in light mode, lighter in dark) until it reads at 4.5:1 on every theme's cards, paper, hovers and its own 5–15 % chips. Status colours have one shade per mode for every theme; each theme sets its own `--amber-text` (light and dark). Worked out by `lib/contrast.ts` (`contrast`, `toOklch` / `fromOklch` with gamut mapping, `textShade`, `wordsIn`, `forWhite`, `onFill`, `WORST`: the darkest light and the lightest dark backgrounds of any theme, Nord's). (2) **Fills with white words** reach 4.5:1: the accent of every theme in both modes (Default and Ink orange 197 81 0, was 232 99 13; the dark themes' accents a step deeper), green 15 134 72 (was 43 151 87), gold 149 111 0 (was 173 131 13), in both modes; in light mode a filled button darkens a little on hover (`--tw-brightness: 0.92`, it brightened under 4.5). (3) **Greys**: light ink-faint 100 94 82 (was 110 104 92, 4.1:1 on Nord's paper-2), dark 162 153 137 (was 4.1 on Nord's surface-2). (4) **Team colours**: `kindColorCss` also writes `--short-text` / `--long-text` for light and dark (`wordsIn`), so a team's yellow still reads. (5) **Inline colours**: `wordColor(c)` (`lib/word-color.ts`): an app colour → its word shade, anything else → mixed toward the ink (`--word-mix`: 65 % in light mode, 50 % in dark); used by the stage pills, the bell's pills, the long videos' themes and stages, mentions in comments, the short type tags; the gold trophy icons use `--gold-text` (#b8860b was 2.6:1); Outliers' formats / keywords use `--green-text`. The short and long-video stage pills sit on the card's colour (`color-mix(… 14%, rgb(var(--surface)))`), not see-through, so they read on the glass wash or a wallpaper. (6) **Avatars**: `PALETTE` (`lib/avatar.ts`) four colours deeper (white initials 4.5:1, were 3.2–4.1); `onFill(color)` picks dark initials on a light colour (people, members, search, notes, the profile picture through `--pf-on`); the bell's grey avatar 6F6A62. (7) **Glass in light mode**: the wash's three fields keep OKLCH lightness ≥ 0.84 (`oklch(from … max(l, 0.84) c h)`, a theme's and your own three colours), so words on the page and on see-through cards read at any level (Ink at 100 % included); dark mode's wash is unchanged. **A wallpaper on every page**: behind a page other than the dashboard (whose words sit right on the picture) the paper over it is at least the 70 % as before, and more for a picture too dark for light mode's dark words or too bright for dark mode's light ones, worked out from its measured brightness so grey words read at 4.5:1 (`.wall-cover[data-words]` in globals.css, `DashboardBackdrop words`, at most 93 %; the dashboard keeps your dim). (8) **TikTok's logo**: a faint light rim and its cyan and red echoes (it vanished on dark cards and the black selected chip). (9) Small ones: Outliers' multiplier number colours (`--m-red-ink`, `--m-yellow-ink`, `--m-green-ink`) a step deeper in light mode and the red a step lighter in dark (4.5:1 on their tints on every theme's paper); the theme picker's previews show the new accents (`components/ui/palette.tsx`, checked against globals.css); the error and offline pages' buttons the deeper orange; a long video's people use the avatars' `PALETTE` (it had its own copy of the old colours); team badges pick readable initials (`onFill`); the staging badge on an opaque tint; Objectives' "reached" in its tooltip and the streak card's label readable in both modes; Posting's violet chip with dark words in dark mode. Checked in the harness with Chromium (`t-contrast.mjs`, every word against the pixels under it; `CLICK=` opens a panel first): the short page (fresh and mixed), Settings → Account and Help, the dashboard, Outliers and the widgets, each in 13 looks (default, Sand, Nord, Ink, Lime, Liquid at 50 and 100, Simple with Ocean, Halo with Berry, a wallpaper, Slate with Liquid, dark, dark Liquid); then Outliers, the short page and Settings over a dark and a bright wallpaper (`wall=dark|bright`, the scene now covers pages like the app does), phones, the public site, and Outliers' video and channel panels: no word under 4.5:1 (3:1 for large ones) after the fixes (before them: the staging badge, a stage pill on Halo's wash, grey words over a dark wallpaper). Tests: `tests/light-contrast.test.ts` (recomputes every theme's shades, fills, greys and chips from globals.css in both modes; team colours from yellow to black; the avatars' colours as initials and as words; the wash rule; each guard proven to catch a planted mistake).
- **1.34.5** (Oct 26 2026): **Graphs you can point at, and smoother Outliers.** (no migration; no new dependency) The owner's message of Oct 26 2026, 12:05, with three screenshots, sent while 1.35.0 (light mode) was under way, so it came first: "thiis part from inside here is useless, get rid of it, we can alraedy see what's posted from the ones below" (Ready to post's tiles), "the views widget should now have the funmctionality that the followers one has too and i want to be able to hover over each part of the graphs and get a popup with the followers directly or views", "when you click on an outlier and try to play the video instantly, the video tries to play, then doesnt play and then you have to click it again" and "this design in outliers must be fixed" (a channel's Latest uploads). (1) **Ready to post** (`schedule-panel.tsx`): the tiles over the card and "2 of 4 posted" are gone (`tileFor`, `TONE`, `jump`, the rows' `#posting-<platform>` ids); each platform's own row already says where it is, its logo ticked when posted (`postedOn`). (2) **Views** (`analytics-widgets.tsx` `ViewsWidget`): Followers' filters (`PlatformChips`: All and each platform with its views of the last 7 days, the number you see when you pick it; arrow keys move between them; outside the widget's link) saved as `settings.platform` (`usePlatformPick`, shared with Followers; `CATALOG.views.settings`, `HAS_SETTINGS`, `WidgetSettings`, `renderWidget`); the number, its change and the line follow the pick (`modules/analytics/lib/day-graph.ts` `viewsWeek(series, days)`, pure: each day up to the last day the picked platforms have all reported, the last 7 of those against the 7 before, as in 1.29.0); the line fills the room under the numbers (`GraphRoom` now gives its size; `ViewsLine` replaces `Spark`), with the first and last day under it; a small widget keeps its line of words to one (all of it on hover) so the line still fits. (3) **A popup for each day** of the Views line and the Followers bars: `useDayPointer(n, indexAt)` (the mouse over a day, or a finger: a tap shows the day instead of following the link, dragging along goes day to day, `touch-action: pan-y` keeps the page scrolling; gone when the mouse leaves, at a touch elsewhere, on a scroll), `DayTip` (a portal on the body, as `.dash-item` clips: beside the day, flipped near the edge, level with the graph's top, kept in the window), the day lit (a guide and a dot on the line, the bar's slot tinted); Views: the day's views ("Not all in yet" / "not in yet" for days YouTube hasn't reported), Followers: "+182 new followers" / "12 subscribers lost" / "No count that day"; with every platform shown, each one's number that day too. `slotAt` / `slotMid` / `pointAt` / `pointX` in `day-graph.ts`. The bars' browser tooltips (`title`) are gone. (4) **Outliers, play on the first press**: the video's panel rendered `VideoPreviewBody` while its numbers loaded and `VideoBody` once they arrived, two components, so React built the player anew and the iframe that had just started was dropped (reproduced in the harness: no iframe and the Play button back after the numbers). Now both states render the same `VideoTop` (`panel-preview.tsx`: the player, `side`, the rest) in the same place, with `PreviewSide` / `PreviewRest` or `VideoSide` / `VideoRest` (`video-panel.tsx`, split from `VideoBody`, `usuals(d)`); and a `Player` built anew right after a press plays at once (`pressed`: a Map of video ids, forgotten 1.5 s after its player goes), which covers the stand-in panel giving way to the panels' code. The old `useEffect(() => setPlay(false), [id])` became a reset during render when the id changes. (5) **Outliers rows** (`VideoRow` in `video-card.tsx`): laid out by the row's own width (`.vrow`, `container: vrow / inline-size`, `@container vrow (max-width: 539px)` in globals.css) instead of the window's `sm:`: under 540px the multiplier is on the picture (`Mult on`, readable on any thumbnail), the right column goes and the picture is 124px, so the title gets the width; a long word wraps (`overflow-wrap: anywhere`) instead of running under the chips; `hideChannel` (the channel's own Latest uploads). Checked in the harness with Chromium (`scenes/widgets.tsx` with Views; `t-graph-tip.mjs`: hover, the last day, leaving, a filter saved, arrow keys, Followers, a phone's taps and the link still working; `t-play.mjs` / `t-play2.mjs` (`scenes/outliers.tsx`, `delay=`): play pressed before the numbers and before the panels' code, the same iframe after, the picture again when opened later; `t-latest.mjs` / `t-rows.mjs`: Latest uploads at 1440 and 1100 wide, dark, a phone, the page's lists unchanged on a computer). - **1.34.1** (Oct 25 2026): **Glass bars that hold together.** (no migration; no new dependency) The owner's list of Oct 25 2026, 22:27 (part 2 of 5), with screenshots: "light mode this bar in outliers is really bad, theres no contrast for liquid glass", "this bar needs a rework, should look the same as the top bar basically", "on 0% seethrough, liquid glass top bar does this, basically goes back to default which isnt good", "theres still this weird thing in the middle that happens on liquid glass", and "analytics, the indicator for the page doesnt show under the text to see where you are". (1) **Outliers' bar** (`[data-shell="subbar"]`): the top bar's glass in every style (`--surface` at `--glass-tint` × 0.9; paper at solid); Liquid's old version was a transparent bar with a faint fade (`::before`, paper at tint × 0.25), so over bright thumbnails its words had nothing behind them. From md (globals.css "Pages' own bars float"): a floating pane 10 px under the top bar, lined up with it (margins −12 px, −16 px from xl, undoing its page padding of 32 / 40 px; padding 14 px), `--glass-edge` border, radius 18 px (Liquid 20 px with `--liquid-rim` / `--liquid-drop`, Halo its ring and glow), `--glass-blur`, `--glass-shadow`; solid at level 0; its tab row's own line hidden inside the pane; over a wallpaper the bars' floor (`--wall-floor`, `--wall-lift`). Phones: a tinted band (Liquid's top bar band still stops at the top bar's edge over it). (2) **The top bar at solid glass**: it floated only when not `data-glass-opaque`, so at 0 % it fell back to the plain full-width bar beside Liquid's floating side bar; now `html[data-glass]` floats at every level (`--shell-top` 72 px), a solid `--surface` pane at 0 % with no blur; the scroll edge band (`[data-shell="edge"]`) works at every level too (what scrolled under the solid pane showed sharp around it and between it and Outliers' bar). (3) **The "weird thing in the middle"**: the edge band began sharply at the page's left edge and had `saturate(1.4)`, which lit the colours behind it into a lighter block between the side bar and the top bar even with nothing scrolling (measured in the owner's screenshot and the harness); now it only blurs (10 px) and fades in from the left (`--edge-in`, 28 px) as well as out at the bottom (`--edge-down`, intersected masks). (4) **Tab underlines**: every row of tabs scrolls sideways (`overflow-x-auto`, which clips the other axis too), and the underline hung 1 px below the row (`-bottom-px h-[2px]`), so it showed half its height or none (a video's Analytics). All 13 now sit inside the row: `bottom-0 h-[2.5px] rounded-t-[2px]` (TabNav, TabOverflow, the skeletons and loading pages, Team, Posting, Outliers, Analytics, its Content and Advanced tabs, a video's Analytics). Checked in the harness with Chromium (`?page=outliers`): Liquid light and dark, Halo dark, Simple, solid glass (still and scrolled), a wallpaper, a phone light and dark; the edge band's ramp beside the side bar measured smooth (it jumped 150 → 126); tab underlines at 1×, 1.25×, 1.5× and 2×. Tests: `tests/glass-bars.test.ts` (the bar's glass in every style, floating, solid, its tab line, the wallpaper floor, its page padding; no tab underline below its row anywhere; each guard proven to catch a planted mistake), `tests/phone-glass.test.ts` updated (floating at every level, solid pane, the edge at every level, its masks, only a blur).
- **1.34.0** (Oct 25 2026): **Pop-ups that leave the way they came.** (no migration; no new dependency) The owner's list of Oct 25 2026, 22:27 (part 1 of 5: then 1.34.1 glass, 1.34.2 Settings → Account, 1.34.3 shorts and the dashboard, 1.35.0 light mode): "i want exit animations for prompts and everything that pops up, like the search bar, prompts, everything, when you click away, there should be the same animation for when it comes in, but in reverse … you must make sure the moment you press outside that you cant click inside the prompt while the animation is playing and everything should be very smooth, and also dont forget to animate the darkened background", and "the navigation bar on mobile, i want a cool animation for it when you switch the page, similar to how apple does it". (1) **Leaving copies**: `lib/pop-ghost.ts` `leaveCopy(root, { lock })`, called by `components/ui/leaves.tsx` `<Leaves>` (display: contents; a layout effect's clean-up runs while React is about to remove it, still on the page): clones the pop-up (typed values, ticks, canvases and scroll positions copied; videos, audio and iframes become empty boxes their size, never a second player or page; `role`, `aria-modal`, `data-confirm`, `data-floating-menu`, `data-tour*`, autofocus and Outliers' panel marks stripped so nothing takes it for an open window), marks it `data-leaving`, `inert`, `aria-hidden`, and, in a microtask once React has removed the original (not after Strict Mode's pretend unmount, nor when its parent went too), puts it back where it was for as long as its parts' own animation-duration, then removes it; `lock` holds the page's scroll while it leaves. Animations off: its parts take no time, so no copy. Works for pop-ups closed by their own state and ones the page unmounts (`{x && <Dialog open>}`). (2) **globals.css "Pop-ups"**: `pop-dim` / `pop-fade` (opacity), `pop-modal` (10px + 0.96), `pop-menu` (6px toward its button + 0.96, `origin-*`), `pop-drop` (from the top), `pop-sheet` (from the bottom), `pop-drawer` (28px from the right; a sheet below md), `pop-card`; each comes in `--ease-out` `backwards` (no transform left behind) and goes out with its own keyframes reversed for as long (`--pop-fast` 180 ms, `--pop` 220 ms, `--pop-slow` 320 ms) with `--ease-leave` (0.4, 0, 1, 1: `--ease-out` time-reversed sat still for half the way out); `[data-leaving]` and everything in it: no pointer events, no other animation. (3) **Converted**: Dialog (lock), ConfirmProvider, AnchoredMenu, Select, DatePicker, ColorPicker, RolePills (`<Leaves as="span">`), MentionInput's four menus, EmojiPicker, WorkspaceSwitcher, Lightbox (lock), ImageLightbox, What's new's card, the bell's panel and dimmer, the month calendar's day peek, the search palette (lock; its stand-in while the code loads keeps a one-way entrance, so the real one replacing it doesn't flicker), the phone's More sheet (lock), Clip's tour, Outliers' side panel (lock) and its Audience selection bar, Analytics' chart pop-up, the shorts' filters and row menus, Keep which shorts, a short's Move to stage, Delete this team, Team's invite search and Transfer ownership, a long video's notes window, the review workspace's note popup and sheet, the scripts workspace's comment popover, Side by side menu, comments panel and its dimmer, the script editor's selection bar, comment sheet, highlight, link, Export and Page width menus, the comment composer and its mentions, link previews and the side window, grammar's card and panel, the sketch studio (lock) and its menus. Unused `sheetup`, `sheetdown`, `drawer-in`, `whats-new-in` removed. `motionReduced()` moved to `lib/motion.ts` (count-up.tsx re-exports it). (4) **The phone's tab bar** (`BottomNavItems`): one `.nav-lens` (slot `--i` of `--n`, `translateX(calc(var(--i) * 100%))`, 0.55 s `--ease-spring`) instead of a marker per tab; the tab you press takes it at once (`pressed`, before the address changes; forgotten after 8 s if no page came), More while its sheet is open (back if closed without going anywhere); its blob stretches as it moves (`nav-stretch`, a remount per move) and the icon hops (`nav-hop`); plain bars: the amber line at the top; Liquid: the soft pill (`--nav-pad` 4px). aria-current still says the page you're really on. Checked in the harness with Chromium (`scenes/pops.tsx`, `t-pops.mjs`): a window (typed text and scroll kept in the copy, the page held still, its OK unpressable while leaving, fading halfway, gone after), a window the page removes, a prompt (its Delete can't answer it while it leaves), a menu, a dropdown, the calendar, a picture, animations off (no copy), Strict Mode (no copy on open), the tab bar plain and Liquid (moves at once, the icon hops, stretches, settles on the tab, More and back); the dashboard scene's lazy pop-ups (`t-lazy.mjs`) as before. Tests: `tests/pop-ups.test.ts` (durations; each part's way in and its exact way back; the copy's guards; 36 pop-ups wrapped; any other one-way entrance must be on the in-page list with a reason, and a stale entry fails; the tab bar; each guard proven to catch a planted mistake).

Checked in the harness (`scenes/rename.tsx`, `t-rename.mjs`): both headings (Save, Enter, Esc, Cancel, unchanged, empty, clicking away, refused and back), both Settings (Save name, Done after a save, typed and closed), read only, a phone. - **1.33.0** (Oct 25 2026): **The ClipTroop website.** (no migration; no new dependency) The owner's plan after the performance patch: "a very intricate website for the cliptroop brand and app, pricing, plans, etc, just so that the app is public, so tiktok will approve it". (1) **Seven public pages** in `app/(site)/` (server pages; the home page's film-set look: Fraunces, the clapper amber and stripes, one dark slate band per page, plain words, no all-caps labels, no script of their own): **Features** (the production as seven scenes, Plan, Write, Make, Review, Package, Post, Measure, each with the home page's live demo or a still frame from `components/site/frames.tsx` (Thumbnail Studio, Outliers), then Research and Every day), **Platforms** (`lib/site/platforms.ts`: each platform's account, what it posts and how, what it reads, every permission with what it's for and whether it's only with Analytics on, how to take access back; TikTok's Direct Post details its review checks: creator info, privacy with no default, comment / duet / stitch, commercial content, Music Usage Confirmation), **Pricing** (`lib/site/plans.ts`, OWNER to confirm: Crew free up to 3 people, Studio $12 / $10 yearly per person per month, Network priced with you; every plan has every feature; monthly or yearly is two radio buttons and CSS `:has()`, no script; In every plan; five questions), **Security** (what protects accounts, the team's work, signing in, the app and the data, each line something 1.31.x or earlier really does; Report a problem with a mailto subject), **Changelog** (`lib/changelog.ts`, the latest 20, `?all=1` for every one; a server page, so the list never goes to the browser as code), **About** (why it exists, what we hold to, the facts as end credits) and **Contact** (the address and a call sheet: what to write about, with each email's subject). (2) **`components/site/site-chrome.tsx`**: `SitePage` (skip link, header, footer; `flush` when the page ends on a dark band), `SiteHeader` (Features, Platforms, Pricing, Security, Changelog; `aria-current`; the theme switch; Sign in; `SiteMenu`, a `<details>` menu on phones that closes on a new page, Escape or a tap outside), `SiteFooter` (Product, Company, Legal; the contact address when set), `PageHead`, `SiteSection`, `FactList`, `SiteCta`, `askHref()` (mailto with a subject, else /contact). The home page (`components/landing/landing.tsx`) and the legal layout now use them (the home page's own header and footer are gone; its trust panel links Platforms and Security, its ending Ask about an account and the plans). (3) Public: `lib/public-paths.ts` EXACT, `PUBLIC_PAGES` (sitemap and robots, home and website first), `BRAND_PAGES_RE` (brand colours whatever a person's theme). (4) **`/.well-known/security.txt`** (RFC 9116, `lib/security-txt.ts`: Contact, Expires half a year ahead worked out per request, Policy, Canonical; the middleware's matcher skips .txt). (5) `NEXT_PUBLIC_CONTACT_EMAIL` now also feeds the website; `NEXT_PUBLIC_COMPANY_NAME` is on App setup (`COMPANY_NAME_SET`: Contact names who runs the app only when it's set). `.ld-stripes-light` (amber and chalk) for stripes on the dark slate. Checked in the harness with Chromium (`scenes/site.tsx`, `t-site.mjs`, `t-site-ui.mjs`): every page on a computer and a phone, light and dark, no console errors, no sideways scroll; the phone menu, monthly / yearly by mouse and arrow keys, the questions. - **1.32.5** (Oct 25 2026): **Speed: long lists.** (no migration; no new dependency) The performance patch, part 6 (the last). Audit of the lists that grow without end: Outliers, Analytics and the bell's History already page ("Show more", 40 or 100 at a time); the **short videos list drew every short the team ever made** (server-rendered, several client components a row: a year of daily shorts is hundreds of rows to send, draw and hydrate, again on every live update), the long videos list every video. (1) **The history** (`modules/short-videos/lib/history.ts`, pure: `HISTORY_DAYS` 14, `HIDE_AT_LEAST` 30, `shiftDay`, `splitHistory(list, today, showAll)` → `{ cutoff, older, hiding, offer, shown }`): posted shorts planned before today − 14 days are left out once there are 30 or more of them; anything not posted (overdue included) and undated always shows. `?past=all` shows them (filters, views and order keep it); a `HistoryBar` says how many and offers Show them / Show only the last two weeks (above the list in schedule order, below it in Latest first). Counts, day counts, the settings window and "scroll to today" still use the whole list. (2) **Every short is read**: `lib/all-pages.ts` (`PAGE_ROWS` 1000, `allPages(page)`): Supabase answers at most 1000 rows to a request (Project Settings → API → Max rows) and cut the rest silently, so a team past 1000 shorts lost its newest ones from the list and the calendar; `listShorts` now reads page by page in a fixed order (date, place in the queue, id). (3) **Off screen, not drawn**: `lib/chunks.ts` (`chunks`, `LIST_CHUNK` 24, `WIDE_CHUNK` 60: whole grid rows at 1 to 4 / 1 to 6 across); the shorts and long videos lists render their rows and cards in groups, each `.list-chunk` / `.list-chunk-cards` (globals.css: `content-visibility: auto`, `contain-intrinsic-block-size: auto 1450px / 1800px`; cards get 12 px of room inside a group, taken back outside, so a lifted card's shadow isn't cut; groups stacked with flex and a gap). Measured in Chromium (software rendering, 1200 rows): the first render 1.4 to 1.8 s → 0.6 to 0.8 s, scrolling as smooth as before (median frame 17 ms); content-visibility on each row instead made scrolling stutter (frames of 0.5 s), hence groups. Everything that opens from a row is in a portal (menus, pickers, dialogs: tested), so nothing is cut off by a group. Tests: `tests/long-lists.test.ts`.
- **1.32.4** (Oct 25 2026): **Speed: measured where it counts.** (0110; no new dependency) The performance patch, part 5: real visitors' Web Vitals. (1) **In the browser**: `components/web-vitals.tsx` in the dashboard layout: `useReportWebVitals` (next/web-vitals) with one module-level callback; keeps LCP, INP, CLS, FCP and TTFB (not FID) by the measure's id; on `visibilitychange` → hidden and on `pagehide` sends what changed since the last send with `navigator.sendBeacon("/api/vitals", Blob application/json)` (a keepalive fetch when it can't): the page the visit started on, without ids (`pageOf`: UUIDs → [id], numbers → [n], /u/name → [name], anything not a plain lowercase word → [x], 6 parts at most), phone or computer (the md breakpoint), the connection's effective type, how it was opened, the version. Nothing about who. (2) **`/api/vitals`** (POST): this site only (Origin), signed in (`getCachedUser`), 30 a minute per person, 4 KB at most, `readVitals()` (lib/web-vitals.ts, pure: the page named again, the rating worked out with web.dev's thresholds, never the browser's word, each id once, 10 at most, values bounded), `upsert(onConflict: "vital_id")` with the service key, and one beacon in 50 deletes what's older than 30 days; before 0110 nothing happens (204). (3) **Developer → Speed** (`speed.tsx` reads `web_vitals_summary(7)` with the service key, `speed-view.tsx` draws it): for each measure the 75th percentile for phones and computers, coloured good / needs work / poor, with loads and the share that was good, what it means and its thresholds; the pages opened most (loads, LCP, INP, CLS, TTFB); "Run migration 0110" before it ran, "No visits measured yet" before anyone used it. Tests: `tests/web-vitals.test.ts` (thresholds, page names, reading a beacon, the component, the route, the tab), `supabase/tests/web-vitals.test.sql`; checked in Chromium (the harness's next/web-vitals stand-in): one beacon when the page is hidden, nothing new nothing sent, a grown CLS sent again under its id, the starting page kept after moving inside the app, phone and computer.
- **1.32.3** (Oct 25 2026): **Speed: pictures and fonts.** (no migration; **new dependency: `sharp`** ^0.35.5, already in the lock as Next.js's optional one, now the app's own) The performance patch, part 4. Audit of every picture a list shows: Thumbnail Studio's pictures (`package-thumbs`, kept up to 1280 px by the `thumbnail` preset) were shown 46 to 300 px wide, and the dashboard, the calendar and a video's page made a **signed link for each on every visit** (one more trip to storage per render, and a new address each time, so the browser downloaded every picture again). (1) **`?w=`**: `lib/picture-size.ts` (`PICTURE_WIDTHS` 160 / 320 / 640, `pictureWidth()` (only those, written the one way), `studioThumb(entry, w)`, `sketchThumb(id, w)`); `lib/resize-picture.ts` (server only: `resizePicture(bytes, width)` with sharp: at most 40 megapixels in, EXIF turned upright, never bigger, WebP q72, null (the original goes out) when sharp isn't there, the picture is animated, already that small, or the copy isn't lighter). `/api/package-thumb/[entry]` takes `?w=`, and now uses `getCachedUser()` (no trip to Supabase Auth per picture) and a limit (600 a minute per person); new `/api/project-thumb/[id]` (an Ideate sketch, read as the person, the same `?w=` and caching). Both `private, max-age=2592000, immutable`. (2) **Where**: the dashboard's tasks and upcoming long videos (160), the calendar (640, loaded only when a video's details open), a video's page Thumbnail Studio card (640), the long videos list (160 in the table, 640 on a card; the first sketch the same way when there's no winner), search (160), Thumbnail Studio's own variations (as kept, at the stable address: the browser keeps them between visits). No signed link is made for `package-thumbs` any more (the mockup library still is). (3) **Fonts**: `next/font` preloads every file of one call on every page, and Fraunces' italic (used by two bits of the home page) was in the same call as the upright: now `frauncesItalic` (`--font-display-italic`, `preload: false`), used by `.ld-italic` / `.ld-scene-mark` in landing.css. Tests: `tests/pictures-size.test.ts` (the widths, the routes, every place, no signed `package-thumbs` link, the fonts; a child with the react-server condition resizes real pictures with sharp: 1280×720 → 320×180 WebP, a sideways phone photo upright, animated and small ones left alone; skipped where sharp isn't installed).
- **1.32.2** (Oct 25 2026): **Speed: less to download.** (no migration; no new dependency) The performance patch, part 3. Measured with esbuild on the dashboard layout's client components (react and next left out): the frame (sidebar, header, bell, search, What's new, the tour, the celebrations) was 360 KB minified / 115 KB gzipped on every page, mostly words it rarely shows (the changelog alone 111 KB, the search catalog and window 58 KB, the bell's cards 18 KB, the achievements' words 11 KB). Now **105 KB / 34 KB gzipped at once**; the rest (258 KB) loads later in its own chunks. (1) **Search**: `components/search/global-search.tsx` is only the box, the button and the shortcuts; the window is `search-palette.tsx` (`SearchPalette`, mounted fresh each time it opens), `lazy()`-loaded, warmed with `whenIdle()` and on pointer enter / focus / pointer down; opened before it arrives: `PaletteLoading` (the same window, empty, with a spinner; Esc closes it). (2) **What's new**: `lib/changelog-meta.ts` (types, `KIND_LABEL`, `compareVersions`, `seriesOf`, `isFeatureUpdateIn(from, to, newVersions)`, pure); `lib/changelog.ts` keeps the list and re-exports them, plus `whatsNewFacts(version)` (the headline, and the versions of that series with something new: all the update card needs, proven equal to `isFeatureUpdate` for every pair of versions); the layout passes it to `WhatsNewHost`; the notes are `whats-new-notes.tsx`, loaded when the window opens (or a button is pointed at, or the card shows), with `NotesLoading` (the real banner, the list's shape) meanwhile. The amber dot no longer checks the changelog (the newest notes are always package.json's version: tested). (3) **The bell**: `notification-cards.tsx` (every kind's words and pictures, `BellCard`, `HistoryDialog`), lazy, warmed when idle and on the bell; `lib/notification-status.ts` (`actionableStatus`, `waitsForAnswer`) shared; the list shows `CardsLoading` placeholders if opened first; History is drawn from the first time it's opened. The list, its realtime updates and everything you do stay in `notification-bell.tsx`. (4) **Clip's tour**: `tutorial.tsx` only decides when it starts (and `startTutorial`, `ReplayTutorialButton`); the steps and bubbles are `tour.tsx` (`TourRun`), fetched as soon as a tour is wanted, shown 900 ms later as before. (5) **Celebrations and XP cheers** start listening once the page is idle (`celebrations-host.tsx`, `xp-cheers-host.tsx`, `useIdle()`); `lib/profile/levels.ts` (levels, tints, frames) is apart from the achievements' words in `progress.ts` (which re-exports it). `lib/idle.ts`: `whenIdle(f, timeout)` and `useIdle()` (one copy of the idle-callback dance instead of three). (6) Each of these sits in a **`LaterBoundary`** (`components/ui/later-boundary.tsx`): if its code can't be downloaded (offline, or a new version went live since the page opened) or it breaks, only that part says so ("Search didn't load. Check your connection, then reload the page." with Reload; the bell's list and What's new's notes the same) or stays away (the tour, the celebrations); the page goes on, and real errors are reported like a page's. Before, any of them breaking took the whole page to the error screen. Tests: `tests/bundle.test.ts` walks the frame's static imports from the layout like a bundler (types and `import()` left out, server actions stop it): none of the heavy modules is on every page, each still loads with `import()`, only the server and the notes read the changelog, and a budget of 380 KB of source for the frame (1.32.1: 814 KB in 98 files; now about 300 KB in 60). Checked in Chromium with the real components: each opens after the page settled, while its code is still arriving and when it can't arrive at all; the tour goes through to the end.
- **1.32.1** (Oct 25 2026): **Speed: the server next to the database.** (no migration; no new dependency) The performance patch, part 2. Audit of the pages' server code: they already ask in one batch (one `Promise.all`, roles loaded alongside); what's left is how long each question takes. (1) **App setup card 11** (`lib/server-speed.ts`, server only: `measureServerSpeed()`: `VERCEL_REGION`, three timed `teams` reads with the service key (the middle one), and `<Supabase>/auth/v1/.well-known/jwks.json`; `lib/regions.ts`, pure: Vercel's regions, Supabase's regions → the Vercel region in that city, `median`, `distanceOf` (≤ 20 ms the same city, ≤ 45 near, more far), `signInKeys` (an EC / RSA / OKP key: "own", none: "shared")). With the server far from the database every question costs about 90 ms; with the old shared JWT secret the middleware's `getClaims()` asks Supabase Auth on every page. The card shows both live, with the steps (Vercel → Settings → Functions → Function Region; Supabase → Project Settings → JWT Keys → Migrate JWT secret, then Rotate keys). (2) **The frame**: `getShellProfile()` (lib/look-server.ts): the profile's 19 columns the dashboard layout needs (name, picture, sounds, animations, palette, the tour, the glass look, the wallpaper) in one read instead of four, with your wallpapers in the same batch (`everywhereFrom`); a database before 0098 reads them one by one as before (`legacyShell`). (3) **The dashboard**: `dashboardProfile()` reads the name, layout and background once instead of three times, and your wallpapers (brightness) come in the first batch instead of a second round. Tests: `tests/speed.test.ts`.
- **1.32.0** (Oct 25 2026): **Speed: the database asks once.** (0109; no new dependency) The performance patch, part 1 (1.32.0 to 1.32.5). Audit of every read rule and every foreign key on a local copy with every migration: the team rules already ask once (0064), but seven read rules still called a check per row (revenue for Masters and the people given access, other income, money projections and their points, who sees revenue, the social history, the daily word), and nine references had no index. 0109 (see Migration history). Tests: `supabase/tests/reads-once.test.sql` (guards proven to catch a planted per-row rule and an unindexed reference).
- **1.31.16** (Oct 25 2026): **Security: what a page may run.** (no migration; no new dependency) (1) **Content Security Policy with a nonce** (Next.js's documented recipe): `lib/csp.ts` (pure) `buildCsp({ nonce, dev, preview, supabaseHost })`: `default-src 'self'`; `script-src 'self' 'nonce-…' 'strict-dynamic' https: 'unsafe-inline'` (the last two only for browsers too old for nonces; +`'unsafe-eval'` in development); `style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'` (never a nonce there: it would switch off every server-rendered style="…"); `img-src` / `media-src 'self' data: blob: https:`; `font-src 'self' data:`; `connect-src 'self' https: data: blob: wss://<this Supabase>` (+`ws:` in development); `frame-src 'self' https:` (players and links opened in the side window); `worker-src 'self' blob:`; `manifest-src 'self'`; `object-src 'none'`; `base-uri 'self'`; `form-action 'self'`; `frame-ancestors 'none'`; `upgrade-insecure-requests` (not in development); Vercel's toolbar on previews. `newNonce()` (16 random bytes, Web Crypto), `cspHeaderName(CSP_MODE)` (`report-only` / `off` for an emergency, else enforced), `wantsCsp(path)` (not /api/*, not /offline.html). The middleware (`lib/supabase/middleware.ts`, the final forwarded response) deletes any `x-nonce` / policy a browser sent, sets a fresh nonce on the request (`x-nonce` and the policy: Next.js reads the nonce from the request's policy and puts it on its own scripts) and the same policy on the answer. The root layout is async and puts `(await headers()).get("x-nonce")` on its first-paint script (`suppressHydrationWarning`: browsers hide a nonce): every page now renders per visit, which a nonce needs. The offline page and the platform sign-in's popup (HTML written by hand) carry their own stricter policies (`default-src 'none'`; the popup's one script by its own nonce). Checked in the harness with Chromium: every scene (dashboard, objectives, settings, Outliers, password, welcome) renders under production's policy with nothing blocked but a planted script without the nonce, and a script loaded by a trusted one (like the Word / PDF export libraries) runs. (2) **next.config.ts**: Permissions-Policy also turns off serial, HID, Bluetooth, MIDI, screen capture and topics; `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin-allow-popups` (the sign-in popup still answers; it also has BroadcastChannel and storage); `X-Permitted-Cross-Domain-Policies: none`; nosniff, X-Frame-Options DENY, HSTS and the referrer policy as before. `CSP_MODE` in .env.example. Tests: `tests/headers.test.ts` (the policy directive by directive, development and previews, the nonce, the middleware and layout wiring, no page built ahead of time, no `<script>` without the nonce, HTML routes have their own policy, every other header).
- **1.31.15** (Oct 25 2026): **Security: forms and what's shown.** (0108; no new dependency) Audit of every raw-HTML sink, every address opened in code, every export and every free-text input. Found: React 19 blocks `javascript:` in href/src/action itself, so links are safe; the five raw-HTML places are escaped or the app's own (lite markdown escapes first, the first-paint script is constant, the print page escapes, the unused pages grid copies the editor's own DOM, the mention box only empties itself); emails and the popup page escape; link previews keep only http(s) pictures. Fixed: (1) **a script picture with a `javascript:` (or any non-web) address**: TipTap kept the pasted `src` as it was, and its Download button's fallback `window.open(src)` would have run it in the app's own window. `pictureSrc()` (`modules/scripts/lib/paste-images.ts`: https / http, or `blob:http(s)` while uploading) now decides what's shown, downloaded (then `https?:` only) and opened full size, and a pasted picture that isn't on the web is left out. (2) **Exports**: `lib/csv.ts` `csvCell` / `csvText`: a text cell starting with = + - @ tab or return gets a `'` (a plain number like -12.50 doesn't); Analytics' `csvDownload` uses it (a search term viewers typed could have been a formula). (3) **Search** opens only the app's own pages in a new tab. (4) **Forms**: `lib/form-limits.ts` (`DB_LIMITS`, the database's; `FORM_LIMITS`; `tooLong(value, max, what)` → "Keep the hook under 10,000 characters."): a new long video's titles (100), theme, sub-theme, hook, notes and budget notes; the video page's type / theme and inline hook / notes / budget (and that each is text); Settings' name (100). 0108 (see Migration history). Tests: `tests/forms.test.ts` (raw-HTML and navigation allowlists with reasons that fail for a new one or a stale entry, no eval / string timers / `javascript:` strings, lite markdown, `pictureSrc` cases and wiring, CSV cells, the app's limits equal the migration's), `supabase/tests/form-limits.test.sql` (the guard, too long refused through the API, the app's sizes accepted, usernames, an old username doesn't block, NOT VALID). Each guard proven to catch a planted mistake.
- **1.31.14** (Oct 25 2026): **Security: the database's own locks.** (0107; no new dependency) An audit of every table (98, all with RLS), rule, database function (215; 187 definer, all with search_path), view (none) and bucket. Found: (a) anon (the public key in every browser) could call 51 functions directly (none answered anything without a sign-in); (b) authenticated had TRUNCATE (skips RLS) / TRIGGER / REFERENCES on every table; (c) **every signed-in account could read every profile, email included** (`using (true)`), and Supabase takes a sign-up from anyone with the public key unless "Allow new users to sign up" is off, so a stranger could list every user's email; (d) the long-video defaults took any member id (the shorts' were checked since 0036); (e) `handle_new_user` still put a new account into an old email invite row; (f) the sketch upload policy never matched (`name` = `scripts.name`); (g) comment attachments took any type (the app stored a page or SVG as a download, the bucket didn't refuse one); (h) `team_today` / `script_step_member_ids` answered for any id. Checked fine: `submit_feedback` keeps only your own team (the note from 1.31.12), no public bucket can be listed, `team_id_for_project` / `review_path_ok` / `long_review_path_ok` answer for any id but the storage and project rules need them and they say nothing useful. Changes: 0107 (see Migration history). App: `lib/profiles.ts` `getProfileByHandle` → `profile_card` (a database without 0107: the old query; `ProfileCard` type, never an email); `searchInvitableUsers` → `people_to_invite` (Masters, `overLimit("invite-search", 300/hour)`, UUID check; before 0107 the old query without the email match); the invite box says "Name, username or their whole email…" and shows a found-by-email person by that email's name part; `ATTACHMENT_TYPES` + `storedTypeFor` (anything not on the list → application/octet-stream; wildcards video/*, audio/*, font/*); App setup card 10 "Supabase: who can make an account" (`lib/auth/sign-up-settings.ts`: GET /auth/v1/settings with the public key, 3 s: disable_signup, mailer_autoconfirm, external.anonymous_users, shown as ✓/✗). Tests: `supabase/tests/database-locks.test.sql` (guards for every table and function now and later: anon nothing, no TRUNCATE etc., RLS everywhere, no-rule tables have no rights, no views past RLS, definer functions set search_path, no `true` rules, new tables closed to anon; then profiles seen by A/B/C/D/F, an invite ending, anon, cards, invite search, the search bar, past members through team and account deletion and leave_team, new accounts, long defaults, buckets; each guard proven to catch a planted mistake), `tests/database.test.ts` (the wiring, global_search identical to 0090 but its people, the bucket lists equal the app's, sign-up settings parsing, card 10).
- **1.31.13** (Oct 24 2026): **Security: the app's own addresses.** (no migration; no new dependency) (1) **Cross-site writes**: `lib/request-guard.ts` `crossSiteWrite({ method, path, host, origin, fetchSite })` (pure): a POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE to `/api/*` (not `/api/cron/*`, not `/api/auth/*`) whose Origin isn't this host (or `null`, or broken), or with no Origin and `Sec-Fetch-Site: cross-site`; the middleware answers 403 before any session work. Server actions already get Next.js's own Origin check; cookies are SameSite=Lax; the push routes keep their own `sameOrigin`. No route sends CORS headers (tested), so no other site can read an answer. (2) **Limits** (`overLimit`, per server instance, 429): link previews 60/min per person, analytics thumbnails 600/min, weather 60/min per address, invites 30/hour per master, Outliers' Add channel 60/hour, bug reports 5 per 10 minutes; grammar (90/min) and error reports (10/min) moved onto the shared limiter (their own maps never forgot anyone); GIF search (40/min) and push tests (one per 15 s) as before. (3) **Machines calling in**: only the timer (`/api/cron/*`, CRON_SECRET, 1.31.12's shared check); no inbound webhooks (tested: the only routes taking writes are cron/posting, errors, grammar, push/subscribe, push/test); the platforms' sign-ins: state from 32 random bytes, used up when checked, 15 minutes, masters and schedulers only. Tests: `tests/api.test.ts`.
- **1.31.12** (Oct 24 2026): **Security: who can do what.** (no migration; no new dependency) An audit of every server action (243 in 35 files), API route (23), cron job and developer page. Findings: every action that uses the service key checks first (sign-in, then the team/role for the row it acts on: `who()` in Outliers, `manager()` in posting, `requireMaster` / `requireSocialManager` / `requireProjectMaster`, `teamTz` / `teamForRequest` in Analytics, the invitee or transfer target in invites, `requireDeveloper` on /developer); the rest use the person's own client and leave it to RLS or a database function that checks the role (listed with reasons in the test); /developer and /setup answer 404 to non-developers; the cron routes need CRON_SECRET (16+ characters, constant-time). Changes: (1) `tests/access.test.ts`, a guard: finds every exported action ("use server") and route, follows the file's own helpers, and fails for one that checks nobody unless it's in `LEFT_TO_THE_DATABASE` / `OPEN_ROUTES` with a reason (those may not use the service key; the one exception, a script comment's drawing removed after RLS deleted the comment, is listed); it also checks the cron routes check first, the developer pages and actions, `switchTeam` and the posting actions' role check. Proven to catch a planted unchecked action and route. (2) `lib/cron-auth.ts` `cronAuthorized(header, secret)` (pure; the routes pass `process.env.CRON_SECRET`), replacing three copies. (3) `switchTeam` sets the team cookie only for a team the person is an active member of (it was checked when read, not when written). (4) `setViewMode` keeps only grid/table and a word-like stage. Left for 1.31.14 (database): the RLS policies these actions rely on (team settings, objectives, long-video columns), `updateLongSettings`' default people being members of the same team, `submit_feedback`'s team being the reporter's.
- **1.31.11** (Oct 24 2026): **Security: sessions.** (no migration; no new dependency) (1) **Log out = this device**: `signOut()` → `signOutHere()` (push cleanup as before, then `auth.signOut({ scope: "local" })`; supabase-js's default is `global`, so Log out used to end every device's session). The "Log out?" text (`LOGOUT_CONFIRM`, shared with the search palette) says other devices stay signed in. (2) **Sign out everywhere else** (Settings → Account, `#sessions`, beside Log out; in search): `signOutElsewhere()` (getUser, `signOut({ scope: "others" })`, then `forgetOtherDevices()`). (3) `lib/push/devices.ts` `forgetOtherDevices(supabase, userId)`: deletes the user's push subscriptions except this device's (`PUSH_COOKIE` = sha256 of its endpoint; none when the cookie's missing); also after `changePassword` and after a reset (`forgetOtherDevicesNow()` from /reset-password), since Supabase signs the other sessions out on any password change. A device signed in again turns its notifications back on by itself (PushKeeper). (4) **Secure cookies**: `lib/supabase/cookie-options.ts` `sessionCookieOptions(protocol)` → `{ secure }` for https (`x-forwarded-proto` on the server, `request.nextUrl.protocol` in middleware, `location.protocol` in the browser), passed as `cookieOptions` to all three Supabase clients (on top of @supabase/ssr's path /, SameSite=Lax, 400 days; not HttpOnly, the browser client reads them); the team and view-mode cookies too (the push and popup ones already were). (5) **An email's link never swaps accounts quietly**: `/welcome` checks `getUser()` first; nobody signed in → `verifyOtp` as before; someone signed in → `verifyOtp` on a side client (`persistSession: false`): same account → `setSession` on the real client; another account → "This link is for another account" with Continue as <link's email> (`signOutHere()` then `setSession`) or Stay as <current> (the side session is signed out at once; the link is used up). Old-style `#access_token` links: `AuthHashHandler` compares the token's `sub` (`tokenClaims`, read not verified) with the signed-in user and, if different, leaves the tokens in sessionStorage (`SWAP_KEY`, 10 minutes, this tab) for `/welcome?swap=1` to ask. Pure parts in `lib/auth/link-swap.ts`. Tests: `tests/sessions.test.ts`. Harness: `scenes/welcome.tsx` (every path, with a supabase-js stand-in), `scenes/password.tsx` (Settings' two buttons).
- **1.31.10** (Oct 24 2026): **Security: passwords.** (no migration; no new dependency) (1) **One set of rules**, `lib/auth/password-rules.ts` (pure, browser and server): `checkPassword(pw, { email, names })` → `{ ok, error, checks }`: ≥ 8 characters (as people count them), ≤ 72 UTF-8 bytes (Supabase/bcrypt keeps no more and refuses longer), not guessable (a list of the most used ≥ 8-character passwords; a common word with only numbers or symbols around it, leetspeak undone, said twice or with up to 3 letters added: Password2026!, p@ssw0rd, mypassword, passwordpassword; ≤ 3 different characters; a run of 6+ along a keyboard row or the alphabet, or steps of one: 12345678, qwertyui, 87654321), not personal (the email, its name part and pieces of 4+, its company domain unless a mail provider, the full name and username, leetspeak undone both ways). No character-class rules (NIST 800-63B). `passwordSaveError(error, "link" | "settings")` maps Supabase's answers (same_password, weak_password with reasons pwned / characters / length, > 72, reauthentication_needed, current_password_*, 429, a missing session). `fromEmailLink(amr)`: otp / recovery / invite / magiclink / email/signup (the token-hash verify on /welcome gives every type `otp`; the hash flow gives recovery / invite). (2) `components/ui/new-password.tsx`: `<NewPasswordFields>` (box, Show/Hide, typed again, the checklist ticking) + `newPasswordState()`. (3) **/reset-password** rebuilt like /set-password: says whose password it is, only for a session from an email link (a password sign-in is sent to Settings: it used to let anyone at a signed-in computer set a new password without the current one, and Supabase then signs every other device out), the rules, a done screen that says the other devices were signed out. **/set-password**: the same fields and rules; a password sign-in starts at About you. (4) **Settings → Account → Password** (`password-section.tsx`, `#password`, in search as Change password): current + new + again; `changePassword` (account-actions.ts): getUser, the rules again on the server, the sign-in limits (0105) before the current password is checked, `signInWithPassword` on the user's own cookie client (verifies it AND gives this device a fresh session, so Supabase's Secure password change is satisfied), `updateUser({ password, current_password })` (Supabase's GoTrue `UpdatePassword` → `LogoutAllExceptMe`: every other session of the account is revoked; refresh tokens stop at once, access tokens last until they expire, an hour by default, but server actions ask getUser() and stop at once); "Email me a reset link" for a forgotten one. (5) App setup → card 9 "Supabase: password rules" (minimum length 8, no required characters, Secure password change on, Require current password if shown, leaked passwords on Pro, the Password changed notification). Tests: `tests/password-rules.test.ts` (31 sample passwords, the 72-byte limit, Supabase's answers, amr, the wiring, and only three places may call `updateUser({ password })`). Harness: `scenes/password.tsx` (reset states, Supabase refusals, Settings with a mocked `changePassword`, the invite's step by amr).
- **1.31.9** (Oct 24 2026): **Phones and glass.** (no migration; no new dependency) The owner's Oct 24 list, the rest. (1) **The top bar floats** with the glass look on a computer (≥ md, not at solid glass): globals.css "The top bar floats (1.31.9)": `top 10px`, `height 52px`, `margin 10px 20px 0` (24 px from xl, the page's own edges: the dashboard's "Good morning" lines up), border `--glass-edge`, radius 18 (Liquid 20 with `--liquid-rim`/`--liquid-drop` and its pointer light on the rim, `glass.tsx` LIT includes the header; Halo its ring colour and glow), `--surface` at 0.9 × tint, `--glass-blur`; over a wallpaper the bars' floor and lift. `--shell-top: 72px` on `<html>` meanwhile: Outliers' bar (`top-[var(--shell-top,3.5rem)]`) and the script editor's header (`UNDER_BAR`, `--script-head-bottom` as a calc) stick under it. **The edge**: `<div data-shell="edge" aria-hidden />` just before the header in the layout: sticky, height 0, z 14 (under a page's bar z 15 and the header z 20), not glass itself (a backdrop-filter on it would make it the backdrop root and its band couldn't blur the page); its `::before` band (`--shell-top` + 10 px, blur 10 px, a little paper, mask fading over its last 26 px, down to Outliers' bar on that page) comes in over the first 48 px of scrolling (`animation-timeline: scroll(root block)` in `@supports`; elsewhere it's always there, over the empty top only). Harness: header x = h1 x in Liquid, Simple and Halo; non-glass and phones unchanged. Also: Outliers' Formats/Keywords detail panes, Saved's folders and Thumbnails' side stuck at a fixed 4.5rem since before Outliers' bar was sticky, so their tops hid behind it; now `top-[calc(var(--shell-top,3.5rem)+var(--ol-bar,104px)+0.75rem)]` (+ max-h to match; `--ol-bar` was already measured, unused since 1.30.0); the centred pages' `min-h-[calc(100vh-57px)]` → 64px (no 5 px scroll under the taller bar). (2) **Toasts**: on a phone the stack spans the screen (`inset-x-0 px-3`) and a toast sits in its middle (`max-w-[420px] mx-auto`; it hugged the left on wider phones); the × is always there (it was hover-only); `useSwipeAway({ lift, onLift })`: a toast goes with a swipe up on a phone (top) or down on a computer (bottom) as well as sideways (`liftOutcome`: two fifths of its height, 24 px at least, or a flick ≥ 0.45 px/ms after 12 px; the card is `touch-action: none`); `toastDuration`: 3.5 s, an error 5.5 s, with Undo 6.5 s (were 4.2 s and 9 s); pointing at them still waits; computer `sm:max-w-md`. (3) **Pictures**: `components/ui/avatar-img.tsx` `<AvatarImg src fallback />` for every person's picture, team logo and platform account picture: loaded eagerly (some iPhones never started lazy pictures inside the page; the circle stayed its colour), `referrerPolicy="no-referrer"`, one retry with `?retry=1` (past a cached failure), then the fallback (initials) — never an empty circle. 20+ places, including the dashboard widgets, word game, review markers, profile showcases, mockups, connected accounts and the posting panel (TikTok and Instagram picture links expire). `lib/video-still.ts` `stillSrc()` adds `#t=0.1` to a video's address (an iPhone draws nothing for a video that hasn't played): wallpaper tiles and the one being made in Settings, the backdrop, profile showcases, feedback attachments; wallpaper pictures load eagerly. (5) **Notifications**: removing was already at once (1.31.4); the card now leaves in 0.2 s and its gap closes 0.08 s after (`LEAVE_MS` 320). Tests: `tests/phone-glass.test.ts` (times, lifts, retries, stills, no bare person picture anywhere, the CSS and wiring), `tests/notifications.test.ts` follows.
