- **1.31.8** (Oct 24 2026): **Outliers, clearer.** (0106; no new dependency) The owner's Oct 24 list, Outliers part. (1) **The first click on a video reloaded the page**: `Panels` was `dynamic()` without `loading` and only rendered once `ctx.panel` was set, so on the first open (before the idle preload finished) it suspended up to the route's loading.tsx and the whole page showed its loading screen until the chunk arrived (reproduced in the harness with the panels chunk delayed: "ROUTE LOADING" for 800 ms on a laptop, 1.6 s on a phone; after: the frame at once). Now `PanelFrame` is in the page's code (`outliers-view.tsx`), `PanelBody` (`panels.tsx`, no frame of its own) is `lazy()` inside `<Suspense fallback={<PanelLoading panel />}>`; `panel-preview.tsx` (main bundle) holds `PanelLoading`, `VideoPreviewBody`, `VideoSkeleton`, `ChannelLoadingBody` and `Player` (moved from video-panel.tsx / channel-panel.tsx, both use them while loading, same `bodyKey`, so nothing jumps). (2) **Tabs keep their scroll**: `toTop()` (instant `scrollTo(0)`) on a tab's click (More's items click the tab) and in `go()` when the tab, niche or kind changes. (3) **The ×**: unchanged math (views ÷ the median of up to 30 same-kind videos posted before it; refreshes scale it with the views), but every "usual" next to it was the channel's median NOW (`channel.usual`), so 14M / "usual 5.6M" / 3.3× didn't add up (14M ÷ 4.2M then). `usualThen(v)` = views ÷ score and `usualsDiffer` (> 15%) in `lib/scores.ts`; the video panel's × tile says "14M ÷ 4.2M", Views "usual 4.2M", the bars' dashed line is that usual, and `ScoreNote` under the tiles says how it's worked out (+ the usual now when it differs); cards say "vs 1.8K usual" (Kinoko's 30×). (4) **Engaged views**: YouTube gives them to a channel's owner only (and since Aug 27 2026 public views count from the first frame, autoplay and hover too); `getVideoDetail` reads the team's own `analytics_content` row (`ownNumbers`: views, engaged_views, avg_view_pct) → `VideoDetail.own`; the panel's last tile is "Engaged views · N% of its views" for the team's own videos (no "would have earned" estimate on those); `ScoreNote` says why other channels' videos have none. (5) **"Your viewers watch it"**: `buildAudience` now adds up per pair (`lib/audience-pairs.ts`: `AudienceReads` keeps each [their video → our video] once at its bigger count, since a video's own copy since posting and the last 90 days from YouTube overlap and used to be summed; `audienceRows` → up to 12 pairs a channel); `outlier_audience.pairs` (0106, `NEWER_COLUMNS` leaves it out before 0106); `getChannelDetail` → `audience.pairs`; the channel panel's `AudienceNote` lists them (three, then Show all): your video's thumbnail and title (→ /analytics/video/youtube/<id>, `leavePanel()`), "N views from <their video>" (an inline link that opens it in the panel), in a box that wraps and spans the panel on a phone (the old pill had `h-7`, its text spilled out). Tests: `tests/outliers-usual.test.ts` (the iPhone and Kinoko numbers, channelNumbers, the wiring, the pair maths), `supabase/tests/outliers-audience-pairs.test.sql` (titles, order, members only, ≤ 12, server-only writes). Harness (scratchpad): esbuild + stand-ins + Tailwind v4 + Playwright, before/after builds from a `staging` worktree.
- **1.31.7** (Oct 24 2026): **Security: the vulnerability gate.** (no migration; no new dependency) CI's "Known vulnerabilities" job went red on staging after 1.31.6 + the owner's `npm update` (commit "vuln change"): `npm audit --omit=dev` found next@15.5.28's own pinned `postcss@8.4.31` (`node_modules/next/node_modules/postcss`): GHSA-qx2v-qp2m-jg93 (moderate, `</style>` XSS in stringify, fixed in 8.5.10), GHSA-6g55-p6wh-862q, GHSA-fxqj-rqcc-2cmp and GHSA-r28c-9q8g-f849 (high, sourceMappingURL file reads; all ≤ 8.5.22). No Next 15 release moves off 8.4.31 and `audit fix --force` wanted next@16.4.3 (a major). Not reachable in ClipTroop (PostCSS only reads our own CSS at build time), fixed anyway: (1) `package.json` `"overrides": { "postcss": "$postcss" }` with devDependencies `postcss ^8.5.31`: Next uses the root PostCSS 8.5.31 (the copy Tailwind already used). The lock was edited by hand (this sandbox can't reach npm): `node_modules/next/node_modules/{postcss,nanoid}` removed, `dev: true` dropped from `node_modules/postcss` and `node_modules/postcss/node_modules/nanoid` (Next ships with them now), root devDependencies updated; checked with `npm ci --dry-run --offline` (537 packages, nothing to fetch = consistent; a package.json change without the lock change fails there with ENOTCACHED) and Arborist (`next → postcss` spec ^8.5.31 resolves to `node_modules/postcss`, 0 invalid edges, one PostCSS). (2) **The gate**: `scripts/audit.mjs` runs before every build (`"build": "node scripts/audit.mjs && next build"`, so Vercel and CI's build job too) and as CI's audit job (renamed "Known vulnerabilities (the build stops on them)"): `npm audit --json --omit=dev` (what ships: stops from moderate) and `--include=dev` (dev-only: stops from high), 3 tries; npm unreachable → the build stops (`AUDIT_UNREACHABLE=allow` in Vercel lets one deploy through unchecked during an npm outage); `::error` annotations on GitHub. Pure rules in `scripts/audit-rules.mjs` (+ `audit-rules.d.mts`): `advisoriesOf` (each `via` object once, by GHSA + package; "holder" packages like next add nothing), `readWaivers` (`security/audit-waivers.json`: `{ "waivers": [{ id: GHSA-…, package, reason (≥ 12 chars), until: YYYY-MM-DD ≤ 90 days ahead }] }`; mistakes stop the build, expired ones let nothing through, unneeded ones are pointed out), `verdict`, `fixText` (a major-version fix points to an override, never `audit fix --force`), `describe` (`postcss (inside next): HIGH · … · GHSA-…`). (3) `tests/audit.test.ts` (Oct 24's npm answer as a fixture, every rule, the real waiver file, the wiring, one PostCSS ≥ 8.5.10 in the lock); `tests/packages.test.ts` follows the CI change; DEVELOPMENT.md → "Vulnerability gate" (what to do when it stops a build). Checked end to end against a fake `npm` on PATH (clean → 0; postcss inside next → 1 with the advisory and fix; dev-only moderate → shown, 0; npm down → 1 after 3 tries, 0 with AUDIT_UNREACHABLE=allow). The dev tree was never audited before: if the first run stops on a build tool, update it or waive it.
- **1.31.6** (Oct 23 2026): **Security: signing in.** (0105; no new dependency) The checklist's sign-in part, first of three (then passwords, then sessions). Audit: passwords are Supabase's (bcrypt, never in the app's database or logs); the sign-in error never says whether an email has an account, nor does the reset form; `next` after signing in is this site's own pages only (the callback adds the origin itself); server actions refuse other sites (Next's Origin check). Found: the app signs in from its server (`app/login/actions.ts`), so Supabase's limits per address (`/token`: 150 per 5 minutes) only ever see Vercel's addresses: no limit per person or per account, and one person trying hard would slow everyone's sign-in (Supabase can take the person's address in `Sb-Forwarded-For`, but only with the new secret API keys and once turned on). (1) **0105** (below) and `lib/auth/sign-in-rules.ts` (pure: `SIGN_IN_LIMITS` 15 min / 8 / 30 / 40, `signInKeys` (sha256 of `cliptroop:sign-in:account:<email, trimmed, lower case>` and of the address), `clientAddress` (x-forwarded-for's first, x-real-ip: Vercel sets both and replaces a browser's), `waitText`, `signInOutcome`: ok / wrong (400, `invalid_credentials`) / busy (429) / unconfirmed / down) and `lib/auth/sign-in-limits.ts` (server only, service key: `signInKeysFor(email)`, `signInWait`, `noteSignIn`; if 0105 isn't there or the database is busy it lets everyone through, with a warning in the logs). (2) **Signing in**: the wait first (the password isn't checked meanwhile; "Too many tries. Try again in 14 minutes." with a Reset your password button that opens the reset form with the email filled in), a wrong password counted, a right one clears its account; Supabase being busy ("Too many sign-ins right now"), not set up ("Open your invite email first") or unreachable ("Couldn't sign you in right now") are no longer "Incorrect email or password" and aren't counted; the email is trimmed; anything absurdly long is refused without asking Supabase. (3) **Deleting an account** checks the password with the same limits (a session someone got hold of can't be used to guess it there) and ends its check's own session (it stayed on the account's sign-ins). Tests: `tests/sign-in.test.ts`, `supabase/tests/sign-in-limits.test.sql`.
- **1.31.5** (Oct 23 2026): **Glass pop-ups.** (no migration; no new dependency) Found while doing 1.31.4: since 1.29.3 this file said every `.glass-pane` uses `--glass-pop`, but `html[data-glass] .glass-pane` (specificity 0,2,1) lost to `html[data-glass] .bg-surface:is(.fixed, …, .shadow-2xl, …)` (0,3,1, `--glass-float`), so with Where: cards the dialogs, Are you sure, the Ctrl K palette, menus (`AnchoredMenu`), `Select`, `DatePicker`, the phone's More sheet and the Outliers audience bar stayed at 0.95 at the middle (measured in Chromium); only Where: everywhere worked. (1) `html[data-glass] .bg-surface.glass-pane { --tw-bg-opacity: var(--glass-pop) }` (0,3,1, after the floating rule): measured 0.68 liquid and 0.75 simple at the middle, 0.36 liquid at the end, solid with glass off. (2) Over a wallpaper: `max(--glass-pop, --wall-floor)` and `brightness(--wall-lift)`, like the bars. (3) `glass-lift` on the four that sit over a dimmed page (`Dialog`, `ConfirmProvider`, `GlobalSearch`, the phone's More sheet): in light mode `brightness(calc(var(--wall-lift, 1) * 1.8))`, so the box is light glass and not grey (the dimmer is 45 to 50% black); dark mode keeps the dimming. Checked in Chromium: every pop-up's computed colour at 50 and 100, liquid, simple, everywhere and off, light and dark, over a busy, a bright and no wallpaper; screenshots of each. Tests: `tests/glass-popups.test.ts` (a small specificity calculator: the pop-ups' rule wins over the floating one and nothing else that sets every floating box outranks it; every glass pane is on `bg-surface`; the four over a dimmed page are lifted; mutation-checked).
- **1.31.4** (Oct 23 2026): **Notifications at once.** (no migration; no new dependency) The owner's message of Oct 23 2026, 08:46: "add glass for notifications and optimistic updates and a cool animation of the notification leaving and the one under taking its place. We should want them to feel instantly responsive even if they don't instantly delete but yeah optimistic updates there. Like likes on a video". (1) **`lib/notification-optimism.ts`** (pure): an overlay of what you did that the server hasn't confirmed (`read`, `gone`, `answered`); `rowWithOverlay` / `withOverlay` lay it over any list (the server's, a refresh, a realtime refetch), so nothing you did flickers back; `putBack` (newest first, never twice); `swipeOutcome` (a third of the width, or a flick of 0.55 px/ms over 36 px in the same direction; locked: always back); `leaveDelays` (40 ms apart, the first 8). (2) **The bell** (`notification-bell.tsx`): open (marks read), Mark all read, clear, Clear all, Accept / Decline all apply at once; the server's answer settles them (the overlay entry is dropped 10 s later) or takes them back exactly (an unread one is unread again; a refused answer takes back only itself) with a toast; `markNotificationRead` / `markAllNotificationsRead` now return `{ error? }`. A card that goes slides out (0.26 s), then its slot closes (`.notif-item` grid rows 1fr → 0fr, 0.14 s later) so the ones under it glide up; Clear all staggers; a new one opens its slot and slides in; realtime deletes of a card already leaving let it finish. Swipe (`components/ui/use-swipe-away.ts`): follows the finger or mouse sideways (inline transform, no render per move), `touch-action: pan-y` keeps vertical scrolling, presses on buttons stay the button's, a drag isn't a click, a pause before letting go isn't a flick, images can't be dragged off; an invite waiting for an answer gives 40 px at most and springs back. Keyboard: the card's words are its button (`.notif-main`, role button: the card holds the X and a button can't hold another), Enter/Space opens, Delete clears, the card shows the focus ring (`:has(.notif-main:focus-visible)`). Motion off: all instant. (3) **Glass**: the panel is `rgb(var(--surface) / var(--glass-tint))` set as the colour (the floating boxes' `--glass-float` rule is more specific than a class, which kept it at 0.95; over a wallpaper: the floor and lift the bars have), each card a brighter pane (light 0.5 white, dark 0.07, liquid rim), unread ones with a soft amber glow. (4) **Toasts** (`toast-provider.tsx`): glass whenever the glass look is on (was "everywhere" only), the same API object every render (`useMemo`; pages that use toasts don't redo work), slide out with their slot closing, a new one opens its slot, swipe or × to dismiss, they wait while the mouse is on them (at least 1.2 s after it leaves). Checked in Chromium: light, dark, no glass, simple and liquid over a busy wall; a phone with real touch events (swipe goes, vertical drags scroll); the server refusing a clear, Mark all read and an answer. Tests: `tests/notifications.test.ts` (mutation-checked).
- **1.31.3** (Oct 23 2026): **Smaller pictures.** (0104; no new dependency) The owner's quick fix of Oct 23 2026, 08:48: "any picture uploaded on the website should be under 5MBs and then we should compress it ourselves as much as possible. 25 is way too much. We can leave like 15 for thumbnails." (1) **`lib/image/limits.ts`** (pure): `PICTURE_MAX_BYTES` 5 MB, `THUMBNAIL_MAX_BYTES` 15 MB, `pictureTooBig(file, kind)` ("beach.jpg is 7.3 MB. Pictures can be up to 5 MB." / "…Thumbnails can be up to 15 MB."), `sizeText`. Used by every place a picture is picked: avatar (Settings and the profile, `use-avatar-upload.tsx`; Set password, at pick time), team logo, thumbnails (15), Thumbnail Studio (15), script pictures, comment attachments (pictures 5 MB, other files still 25 MB; the menu's "≤25 MB" says so on hover), drawings, wallpapers (still pictures you pick; not one being made lighter, and GIFs and videos keep their own path), reports (`feedbackFileProblem`: pictures 5 MB, videos 25 MB). (2) **`lib/image/compress.ts` presets** (the most each is ever shown, ×3 for a sharp phone): avatar 320 px on its short side (`cover`, shown cropped) at 0.82, logo 256 px short side at 0.86 (see-through kept), thumbnail 1280 px on its long side at 0.86 (YouTube's own size; a Short's cover is tall), attachment 1920 px at 0.80 (was 512 / 512 / 1920×1080 / 2400 at 0.85 to 0.90; the unused `background` preset is gone); a JPEG is always redrawn, even when that isn't smaller, so a phone photo's hidden data (where it was taken) never goes up. Measured in Chromium, a 5.1 MB 4032×3024 photo: avatar 427×320 14 KB, logo 341×256 12 KB, thumbnail 1280×960 79 KB, attachment 1920×1440 139 KB; a 4 MB 1920×1080 PNG thumbnail: 1280×720 176 KB. (3) Newly made small: **Thumbnail Studio** uploads `compressImage(file, IMAGE_PRESETS.thumbnail)` (its own extension), while `registerVariant` keeps the original's width, height and size, so "Under 1280px" and "Over 2 MB: YouTube won't take it" still judge the file you made; **reports' pictures** are made small in `uploadOne` (the item's file swapped, so progress is right, and the report records the type and size that went up); **drawings**: 1200 px, WebP at 0.82 (JPEG, or PNG for see-through, where WebP can't be made), was 1400 px PNG / JPEG 0.86 inside the drawing's data. (4) **0104**: the buckets refuse bigger files too. Tests: `tests/pictures.test.ts`, `supabase/tests/public-files.test.sql`.
- **1.31.2** (Oct 23 2026): **Security: packages.** (no migration; Next.js 15.5.28 by the owner, below) The checklist's "remove unused packages" and "update dependencies". This session can't reach npm's registry (policy), so the audit was done on `package.json` and the lock, and the update itself is the owner's two commands. Audit: all 31 listed packages are used (`postcss`, `tailwindcss`, `autoprefixer` by `postcss.config.js`; `docx`, `pdf-lib`, `@pdf-lib/fontkit` copied into `public/vendor` by `scripts/copy-vendor.mjs`; the rest imported); all 542 packages in the lock come from `registry.npmjs.org` with a sha512 checksum; only `fsevents` (Macs) and `unrs-resolver` (lint) run an install script; `sharp` is 0.35.5 already (the Oct 14 image optimizer advisory needs 0.35.5+). Next.js 15.5.27 has the Oct 14 2026 advisories (nextjs.org/blog/nextjs-security-update-october-14-2026, fixed in 15.5.28): GHSA-p3m5-59mp-jv5r, high, Denial of Service with Server Actions (this app has 35 server action files: affected), GHSA-673q-mjm6-r5xr, critical, RCE in `use cache` functions (no `use cache` here: not affected), CVE-2026-96889, critical, image optimizer with SVG (sharp 0.35.5 already, and 1.31.1 limits the optimizer to the app's own pictures). Added: (1) **`.github/dependabot.yml`**: npm weekly (Mondays 06:00 Bucharest), minor and patch grouped into one pull request, majors ignored (done by hand), into `staging`; GitHub Actions monthly, into `staging`. (2) **CI job "Known vulnerabilities in what the app ships"**: `npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high` (red until next is 15.5.28). Tests: `tests/packages.test.ts` (every listed package used; lock version 3 with the app's version; the lock lists exactly package.json's packages and ranges; registry and checksum for every package; install scripts only from the reviewed list; Dependabot and the audit job). Later: ESLint 8 is past its end of life (dev only); moving to ESLint 9 means a flat `eslint.config.mjs`; `@supabase/ssr` 0.5.2 is behind (Dependabot will offer it).
- **1.31.1** (Oct 23 2026): **Security: nothing left open.** (0103; no new dependency) The checklist's "check for exposed files" and "debug mode". Audit, nothing to fix: no `console.log` / `debug` / `info` / `table` or `debugger` anywhere in the app (only `console.warn` / `error`, one of them in the browser, for a failed export); no source maps for the browser (`productionBrowserSourceMaps` unset), `poweredByHeader: false`, no debug flags; the 0019 debug function was already dropped in 0022; no test or debug routes (`/api/push/test` is Settings' "Send me a test": same origin, signed in, your own devices, one every 15 s); the error screens (`components/ui/error-screen.tsx`, `app/global-error.tsx`) show a calm message and the digest, never the error; browser error reports carry only the path; server actions answer with plain messages (raw database errors only for the `P0001` messages written for people); `public/` holds only icons, the PDF fonts and their licence, the share picture, the service worker, TikTok's verification file and the three vendor builds; the service worker keeps only the offline page; robots allow only the public pages; `/setup` and `/developer` are developers only (404 otherwise); public buckets use random file names (`crypto.randomUUID()`) and none can be listed. Fixes: (1) **`lib/public-paths.ts` `isPublicPath`**: the middleware's public list as exact addresses plus two folders (`/api/auth/`, `/api/cron/`); it used `startsWith("/login")` and `startsWith("/api/auth")`, so any future page named like `/login-history` or `/api/authors` would have opened without signing in. (2) **`/api/health`**: one check serves every call for 20 s per instance (calls during a check wait for it), `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`; each call used to run a database query, an auth call and a storage call. (3) **`next.config.ts` `images.remotePatterns`**: only this app's own Supabase host (from `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` at build); `*.supabase.co` let anyone resize any project's pictures on the app's bill (the app doesn't use `next/image` today). (4) **Public files** (0103 + `lib/image/compress.ts`): `PICTURE_TYPES` / `isPictureType` (the same list as the buckets), `compressImage` turns an SVG into a PNG (drawn as a picture, so its scripts never run; up to 4× for crispness within the preset), and the five picture uploads (avatar in Settings and on Set password, team logo, thumbnails, script pictures) check the type first with a clear message ("Use a picture: PNG, JPG, WebP or GIF."); comment attachments go up with `storedTypeFor(type)`: a web page, SVG, XML or script (or no type) is stored as `application/octet-stream`, a plain download. Tests: `tests/exposed.test.ts`, `supabase/tests/public-files.test.sql`.
- **1.31.0** (Oct 23 2026): **Security: keys stay secret.** (no migration, no new dependency) The first of the security patch from the owner's TikTok checklist (Oct 23 2026, 00:02: "hide my API keys", "check my env variables", "check git for secrets"). Audit: every setting the code reads (`process.env.X` and `env("X")` in `lib/social/providers.ts`): no secret is `NEXT_PUBLIC_` (only the Supabase anon key and the VAPID public key are, by design); every module that reads one is `import "server-only"`, a route or a `"use server"` file; nothing passes one to a browser component (`/setup` shows only whether each is set, developers only); no key is written anywhere in the code; all 118 commits on both branches scanned again (Google, Supabase, GitHub, Slack, AWS, Stripe, Resend, Meta, OpenAI / Anthropic shapes, private keys, JWTs, secret names given a value): none, and `.env.example` is the only env file ever committed; sealed sign-ins (`social_accounts.*_token_enc`) are outside the browser's column grants (0039); vault secrets are read only by functions the browser can't call (`run_posting_tick`, `posting_call`) or that never return them (`posting_health` says only whether they exist); `CRON_SECRET` is required (16+ characters) and compared in constant time on all three cron routes; client error reports carry only the path. Fixes: (1) **`.gitignore`**: every `.env*` except `.env.example` (`.env.production` and the like were not ignored), plus `*.key`, `*.p8`, `*.p12`, `*.pfx`, `*.jks`, `*.keystore`, `service-account*.json`. (2) **`lib/social/crypto.ts`**: AES-256-GCM with `authTagLength: 16` both ways, and a sealed value whose nonce isn't 12 bytes or whose tag isn't 16 is refused (Node would take a tag as short as 4 bytes, making a forged value far easier to slip through). Same format, every stored sign-in still opens. (3) **`/api/giphy/search`**: signed in only (it relied on the middleware alone), `overLimit("giphy", user, 40 a minute)`, the search cut to 50 characters, an 8 s time limit, the key put in with `URLSearchParams`, only `https://…giphy.com/` pictures sent back, the browser keeps an answer a minute (`private`). (4) **`lib/rate-limit.ts` `overLimit(bucket, key, max, windowMs)`**: a sliding window in memory per server instance (at most 5000 keys a bucket), for the endpoints of 1.31.5. (5) **`.env.example`** now describes every setting the code reads (`YOUTUBE_API_KEY` with "restrict it to YouTube Data API v3", the `OUTLIERS_*` shares, `GIPHY_API_KEY`, `LIVE_MINUTES`, `FX_RATES_URL`). (6) **GitHub Actions**: `permissions: contents: read` on both workflows, `persist-credentials: false` on every checkout, and the Database workflow's `baseline_through` reaches its script as an env variable (checked: 4 digits), never pasted into it. Tests: `tests/secrets.test.ts` (no secret `NEXT_PUBLIC_` name; server-only readers; every setting described; no value for a secret in the example; the ignore rules; key shapes in 300+ files; the sealing; GIF search, writing suggestions and cron checks; the workflows; `overLimit`). Later, maybe: pin the Supabase CLI version the Database workflow downloads (it takes the latest release).
- **1.30.1** (Oct 23 2026): **Remove a channel from the top, and rows that fit.** (0102; no new dependency) The first part of the owner's message of Oct 23 2026, 00:02 (screenshots of the niche dialog, a video's panel and two rows with ‹ › arrows at their ends). (1) **Remove from a niche** (`modules/outliers/components/remove-from-niche.tsx` `RemoveFromNiche`, in the headers of `VideoPanel` and `ChannelPanel`, after Save): masters and researchers, only for the team's niches the channel counts in (a video: `VideoDetail.niches`, exact since 0102; a channel: `countsIn(d.niches)`); straight from `ctx.nicheId` (or the only one), else an `AnchoredMenu` of them; `useConfirm` (danger), then `setChannelHidden`, then a toast with Undo; on phones an icon (a folder with a minus). `setChannelHidden` now also takes a channel with no row in that niche (its videos came in by the niche's searches, possible when a niche is at its 400 channels): hiding it writes a hidden `search` row with the admin client, after checking the niche is the team's (`ignoreDuplicates`, then hidden = true, so it never overwrites a row someone just added). The panels' caches moved to `modules/outliers/components/detail-cache.ts` (`videoDetails`, `channelDetails`, `nicheChannels`, `forgetChannel`): a change updates the panel on screen and its cache entry (also after the panel moved on, for a toast's Undo) and drops the channel's other cached entries and that niche's cached channel list, so they're read again. `ChannelBody`'s own `hiddenIn` / `added` state is gone: the channel's niche list changes in place with the pure `changeNiche` (`modules/outliers/lib/membership.ts`, with `countsIn`). (2) **The niche dialog's channels**: placeholder chips (`skeleton`, as many as `groupN`, up to 24) until they're read; a cache per niche (`nicheChannels`) shows them at once the next time while they're read again (what was taken out or added meanwhile, `edits`, is kept); a search box over them past 12 (Esc clears it), the first 40, then Show all. (3) **Rows that slid sideways on a computer** (nothing in the app draws ‹ › arrows, so the fix is that nothing slides): Saved's folders wrap from `sm` (a column from `lg` that's no longer a scroll box, so a new folder's name box fits and its focus ring isn't cut off); Thumbnails' colours wrap (the picked one's ring shows whole; names capitalised). (4) **`components/ui/tab-overflow.tsx` `TabOverflow`** (the last child of a tab row's `<nav className="relative …">`): on a computer (`(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`), tabs that don't fit get `data-overflow` (globals.css: absolute, invisible, still measured) and go under More, whose menu clicks the real tab (so its own onClick runs); More shows the current tab's name when it's under it. Pure fit: `lib/tab-fit.ts` `tabsThatFit`. Phones: the row slides and the current tab is scrolled into view. On Outliers' and Analytics' tabs (Outliers' are `sm:px-3 2xl:px-3.5`, so all ten fit from 1280 with Windows scroll bars). A sweep of every Outliers tab from 1024 to 1920 wide, with and without Windows scroll bars, finds nothing that slides sideways. Tests: `tests/outliers-remove.test.ts`, `supabase/tests/outliers-video-niches.test.sql`.

A pill (`Pill`) is a rounded button with ▾; set, it shows its value (amber tint) with an × (`clearPill`); it opens an `AnchoredMenu` with `role="dialog"` (new prop) under it: lists of `Option`s (radios, close on pick: Where with a line each and Tracked disabled without tracked channels, Posted, Channel size, Length) or a `Box` of chips plus an "Up to" `Select` (Outlier score, with a colour dot per step from `heat()`; Views), which stays open. Keyboard: the choice that's on (or the first) is focused on open, ↑ ↓ ← → move, Tab cycles inside, Esc closes and focuses the pill (skips what a `Select` already handled). On phones the bar scrolls sideways (`no-scrollbar`, `overscroll-x-contain` via the class) and each menu is full width. More filters opens a `Dialog` (sm:max-w-xl) with `MoreFilters`, "Clear these" (`clearMore`) and "Show N videos". (2) **Pure** `modules/outliers/lib/filter-bar.ts`: `pillValue` (what a set pill says: "Tracked channels", "Score 5×+" / "Score 2× to 10×" / "Score up to 10×", "Last 30 days", "100K+ views", "10K–100K subscribers", "10–20 min", "Over 2 min"…), `clearPill`, `MORE_KEYS`, `moreCount`, `moreChips`, `clearMore`, `SUB_RANGES`, `SHORT_LENGTHS`, `lengthsFor`. (3) `filter-panel.tsx` is now `MoreFilters` (only the less used groups; `Chip` and `LANG_NAME` still exported); the old column (Where `Segmented`, × usual, Posted, Views, Channel size, Length) and the phone "Filters" sheet are gone, and so is the chips row under the search (the pills show those). `search-tab.tsx` is one column: the search row (words, Search, sort, layout), the bar, the count, results at full width (`VideoGrid` fills by itself). (4) **`AnchoredMenu`** follows its button when anything around it scrolls (one `placeMenu` per frame) and closes only once the button is off the screen (it used to close on any scroll: a phone's pill row sliding into view closed the menu it had just opened). Tests: `tests/outliers-filter-bar.test.ts`.

- **1.29.10** (Oct 22 2026): **Scroll bars that fit.** (no migration, no new dependency) The owner's "the scrollbar doesn't fit in a bunch of places" (Oct 22, 19:00). Cause: `.styled-scroll`, `.widget-scroll` and `.side-scroll` set `scrollbar-width` / `scrollbar-color` next to their `::-webkit-scrollbar` rules; since Chrome 121 those standard properties win and switch the `-webkit-` ones off (also through inheritance: `scrollbar-color` is inherited), so Windows drew its native bar with arrow buttons and square ends, in the theme's colour, over rounded boxes. (1) **One system** (globals.css "Scroll bars" block): `::-webkit-scrollbar` 10 px, a 4 px rounded grey thumb (`--ink` 0.2; 6 px and 0.36 under the pointer), no buttons, transparent track and corner, for every scroller and the page; `.styled-scroll` insets the track 10 px from the ends (rounded boxes, popups, menus; 1.29.9's `.fine-scroll` folded into it); `.widget-scroll` 8 px, faint (0.1) until its box is pointed at; `.side-scroll` 8 px, hidden until pointed at; `.no-scrollbar` hidden, now with `overscroll-behavior-x: contain`. Chrome only restyles a bar when its box's own style changes, so `.widget-scroll:hover` / `.side-scroll:hover` also set `--bar-hover`. The standard properties are only in `@supports not selector(::-webkit-scrollbar)` (Firefox: thin, grey). (2) **Only where bars take room**: the root layout's first script measures a hidden 80 px box with `overflow:scroll` and sets `<html data-bars="classic">` when its bar takes room (Windows, Linux, a Mac set to always show them); every `-webkit-` rule is under `html[data-bars="classic"]`, so phones and a Mac's floating bars stay native. `html { color-scheme: light }`, `html.dark { color-scheme: dark }` (native bars, date and colour pickers dark in dark mode). (3) **Containers**: every `overflow-y-auto` now says `overflow-x-hidden` (20 more places: menus, lists, popups, the phone More sheet, script and grammar panels, sticky Outliers details…), so a too wide child can't make it scroll sideways; rounded ones without a class got `.styled-scroll` (Shorts filters, mention list, chart tooltip list, the phone More sheet, a video's numbers table, Developer's stack traces); tables and strips that scroll sideways got `overscroll-x-contain`. Tests: `tests/scrollbars.test.ts` (no standard properties outside the Firefox block, every `-webkit-` rule behind `data-bars` and never the accent, no buttons, `color-scheme`, the layout's probe, no inline scroll bar styles, every up-and-down scroller sets its sideways overflow).
- **1.29.9** (Oct 22 2026): **Outliers: a steadier panel and clearer numbers.** (no migration, no new dependency) The first part of the owner's Outliers list of Oct 22 2026, 19:00 (screenshots: the Start menu behind the idea, "No videos match" while Beauty and fashion filled, the panel re-opening, the multiplier in the theme's colour, the niche picker's scroll bar). (1) **Menus over popups** (`components/ui/anchored-menu.tsx`): z 125 (was 70: under `Dialog`'s 110, so the idea window's Start menu opened behind it); opens UP when it doesn't fit below and there's more room above, placed by `lib/menu-place.ts` `placeMenu` (a guess of 220 px first, then its drawn height, before it shows); Escape is caught first (capturing) and marked handled, so it closes the menu and not the `Dialog` / Outliers panel it came from (skipped while focus is in a `[data-floating-menu]`, e.g. a `Select` inside it); new `maxHeight` prop; the panel has the new `.fine-scroll` bar. (2) **Finding, not "No videos match"** (`bits.tsx` `FindingCtx`, `FindingNote`; `EmptyNote` `finding` / `findingAction` / `ghost`): `Shell` gives the niche's name while it's being filled (`filling` / `exploreKicked` / `collecting` and under 30 videos); the data-empty notes of Search, For you, Ideas, Formats, Keywords, Title lab, Thumbnails and Channels (all, no words) show "Finding channels and videos for X…" instead (Search and For you with faint cards under it); the blue line at the top hides itself then (`.ol-page:has([data-finding-note]) .ol-finding-banner`). (3) **One panel frame** (`panel.tsx` `PanelFrame` + `PanelContent`; `panels.tsx` `Panels`, one dynamic chunk for both kinds): the frame (backdrop, drawer, bar with back and close, scroll lock, focus, Esc) stays mounted while `ctx.panel` changes type; `VideoPanel` / `ChannelPanel` render `PanelContent` (label, the bar's middle through a portal into the frame's slot, `bodyKey` `v:id` / `c:id`); a new key scrolls the body to the top and fades the content in (Web Animations, 220 ms, none with motion off); before, each kind had its own shell, so going video → channel → back unmounted it and the drawer slid in and the backdrop faded in again. `openChannel(id, preview?)` (context.tsx `channelPreviews`, like `previews` for videos): every button that opens a channel passes its title, picture and subscribers, so the channel panel's bar and header show them at once (laid out where the loaded header goes) instead of "Opening…". (4) **Multiplier colours** (`modules/outliers/lib/heat.ts`, re-exported by `bits.tsx`; tokens `--m-red`, `--m-yellow`, `--m-green`, `--m-green-2` with `-ink` and `--m-on-*`, brighter in `.dark`): below 0.8× red, 0.8–2× quiet, 2–5× yellow (dark number), 5–10× green, 10× and up deeper green, 25× and up a glow on the badge; `Heat.on` is the number's colour on a fill (`Mult`, For you's example thumbnails). Nothing in the scale uses `--amber`. (5) `.fine-scroll` (globals.css): 10 px lane, 4 px rounded thumb inset 10 px from the ends, wider and darker on hover, no buttons; drawn with `::-webkit-scrollbar` in Chrome / Safari, which ignore those as soon as `scrollbar-width` / `scrollbar-color` are set (also inherited: Windows then draws its own bar with arrow buttons), so the standard properties are reset there and only set for Firefox. Search's filter column scrolls only up and down now (`overflow-x-hidden`: a too wide row made it scroll sideways, which Chrome answered with its back / forward arrows). Tests: `tests/outliers-ui.test.ts` (no theme colour in the scale, each band's colour, every token in light and dark; where a menu opens).

- **1.29.4** (Oct 22 2026): **Light wallpapers, on every page if you like.** (0098; no new dependency) (1) **Made small before upload** (`lib/wallpaper-plan.ts` pure + `tests/wallpaper-plan.test.ts`; `lib/wallpaper-convert.ts` in the browser): `planFor(type, size, head)` → image | animated | video | keep | refuse (inputs JPG, PNG, WebP, AVIF, GIF, MP4, WebM, MOV up to 200 MB; `gifTiming`, `isAnimatedWebp`, `isAnimatedAvif`, `isAnimatedPngBytes`); pictures → WebP at the first of 2560×1600 … 1280×800 × quality 0.86 … 0.5 that fits 2 MB (JPEG where WebP can't be written; a small JPEG / WebP that already fits stays); videos and moving pictures → canvas + `MediaRecorder` WebM (VP9, VP8) at `bitrateFor(seconds)` (72 % of 2 MB over the length, 250 kb/s to 2.5 Mb/s), 1920×1080 or 1280×720 past 15 s, the first 30 s (`trimmed` says so), frames from `ImageDecoder` where there is one, else the image animating on the page; `webmWithDuration` writes the Duration into the recording's Info (MediaRecorder leaves it out). Checked in headless Chromium: a 28 MB 6000×4000 JPEG → 1.8 MB WebP (2400×1600) in 1.8 s, a GIF → 4.0 s WebM (length readable), a 6.4 MB 12 s 1080p WebM → 0.96 MB, a 45 s video → the first 30 s at 720p, 1.8 MB (bitrate lowered after that). (2) **Settings → Wallpaper** (`dashboard-background.tsx`): the tile converts with its own progress ("Making a small video 32%", stop button), then uploads; sizes from storage `list` ("Picture · 27 MB"), Make it lighter on files over 2 MB (fetch, the same steps, `replaceWallpaper`; checked: "Made lighter: 27 MB to 1.7 MB."); On every page (Off / On). `DASH_BG_MAX_BYTES` 2 MB, `DASH_BG_ACCEPT` adds MOV. (3) **On every page**: `getEverywhereBackground()` (`lib/look-server.ts`, two reads side by side) in the layout's Promise.all; `EverywhereBackdrop` (`data-global-bg`) inside a `contents wall-tone [data-dash-bg]` wrapper round `{children}`, darkened to at least `EVERYWHERE_DIM` 70 % off the dashboard (smooth); the dashboard page skips its own then; `html:has([data-own-bg]) [data-global-bg] { display: none }`; ordinary cards over it keep 0.8 of their pane with glass. (4) **No flashes**: `DashboardBackdrop` layers a new picture under the old one and fades it in once loaded; `ProfileView` shows the saved background from the moment Save is pressed (it went back to the server's old one until the refresh); `.motion-page > [data-dash-bg]` fades instead of rising (the transform made the fixed wallpaper fill only the page's box). Tests: `tests/wallpaper-plan.test.ts`, `tests/dashboard-bg.test.ts` (2 MB, MOV), `supabase/tests/wallpaper-everywhere.test.sql`.
- **1.29.3** (Oct 22 2026): **Liquid glass reworked, a third style, a tidier Preferences.** (0097; no new dependency) (1) **Liquid** (`globals.css`): no `--liquid-sheen` or white inner glow (the white band along the top of every pane), `--liquid-rim` = a hairline on the top edge + a faint all-round line + a bottom shade, `--glass-edge` 0.2 / 0.07, softer drops, wash `saturate(1.3)` only; the top bar's `::before` fade carries `--glass-tint × 0.6 → 0.25` of the paper (was 0.66 → 0.4: the white strip), the subbar likewise. (2) **The light**: `LiquidLight` now sets `--mx` / `--my` on the pane under the pointer only (`closest('.dash-item, .glass-pane, [data-shell=bottom-nav]')`, one rAF); the rim (`::after`) and a faint light under the words (`::before`, z-index -1) show on `:hover` only, in `--glint-rgb` (the wash's first colour; the theme's accent by default); none on touch. (3) **Halo** (`lib/look.ts` style 'halo', 0097, first-paint script): `--halo-a / b / c` from the wash (base and neighbours via relative colours, or the three of a blend / your own), a 1px gradient ring (`::before` with a ring mask) brighter on hover, a glow of `--halo-a`, tinted borders on ordinary cards, the side bar and the tab bar; its own tint curve. Settings shows three style cards (`STYLES`, a 3-column grid; on phones just the names). (4) Every `.glass-pane` (dialogs, the Ctrl K palette, the bell, menus) uses `--glass-pop` in both scopes (they were `--glass-float`, nearly solid); at level 0 the bars are solid (`data-glass-opaque`). (5) **Settings → Preferences**: four section cards, Mode and colours (`#look`: `#appearance` Light or dark, `#colours` Colour theme, 6 a row on wide screens), Glass (`#glass`, its title is the section heading), Wallpaper (`#background`), Motion and sound (`#motion`: `#animations`, `#sounds`), with a jump row on top; the skeleton matches; search catalog titles and words updated (Light or dark mode; Glass mentions Halo and the colours behind). Checked in the render harness: Liquid light and dark with the pointer on one card (only that card's rim lights, in the theme's colour), Halo with Sunset / Ocean / Cherry over the wash and a wallpaper, the palette at 50 and 100, the style picker on a computer and a phone. Tests: `tests/look.test.ts` (Halo), `supabase/tests/glass-wash.test.sql`.
- **1.29.2** (Oct 21 2026): **Glass on means glass, off means solid.** (0096; no new dependency) (1) **Off is solid**: every `[data-dash-bg]` rule now needs `html[data-glass]` (dashboard widgets, a profile's showcases and header `.prof-frost`, the Customize bar `.dash-tool`); with glass off the bars are solid too (`html:not([data-glass])` header / subbar paper, bottom nav surface, no blur; the phone top bar over a wallpaper), and Settings' wallpaper tiles draw solid cards. (2) **The slider** (`globals.css`): `--glass-tint` = 1 at level 0, a curve through 0.56 (Simple) / 0.46 (Liquid) at 50, 0.04 / 0.03 at 100; float / pop and blur follow; level 0 adds `data-glass-opaque` (`--glass-blur: none`, nothing blurred at all). (3) **The colours behind the glass** (`lib/washes.ts`: `THEME_WASHES` (20 themes' accents, light and dark; the Ink variants share their colour's), `BLENDS` (Sunset, Aurora, Candy, Ember, Tide, Meadow), `washVars`; `lib/look.ts` `wash` / `washColors`, `lookAttributes` → `data-glass-wash` none | one | three and `--wash-light` / `--wash-dark` / `--wash-1…3`; `--wash-base` feeds `body::before`; `vp-look.paint` for the first-paint script, which validates names and values). Settings → Glass: Colours behind the glass Off / On (Off and On again brings back the last choice), a swatch grid (`WashPicker`, `.wash-swatch`) with Your theme, the themes, the blends and Your own (three colour pickers, saved once you stop, like the slider). `getLook` falls back without the new columns (`washStored: false`: the device's colours are used) and `setLook` saves the rest, saying the colours stay on this device until 0096. (4) **Readable over any wallpaper**: `DashboardBackdrop` takes `luma` and puts `--bg-luma` / `--bg-dim` on `<html>` (a `<style>`, there from the first paint; measuring later sets it inline), so the bars read the picture too. `--wall-lift` = clamp(1, 0.74 / bg_eff, 2.2) in light mode, clamp(0.35, 0.26 / bg_eff, 1) in dark (the backdrop's `brightness()`), `--wall-floor` keeps up to 0.45 / 0.3 of the pane over very dark / very bright pictures, `--ink-soft` / `--ink-faint` a step stronger on glass over a picture; 1.29.1's fixed light-mode rules and `--mismatch` are gone. Checked in the render harness: glass off / Simple 50 / Simple and Liquid 100 over dark, bright and striped pictures in both modes, every wash, level 0. (5) Settings → Glass rows stack on phones. Tests: `tests/look.test.ts` (washes, attributes, columns), `supabase/tests/glass-wash.test.sql`.
- **1.29.1** (Oct 21 2026): **Clear your notifications, and small fixes.** (0095; no new dependency) The first of the owner's Oct 21 21:20 list, one version each. (1) **Notifications**: the bell lists only the ones not cleared (`loadBell` in `lib/notification-select.ts`, used by the layout; a database without 0095 lists them all, `missingColumn` tells a missing column from other errors: 42703 / PGRST204). The X on a row (`clearNotification` → `lib/notification-clear.ts` `clearOne`; not on an invite or ownership request still waiting for an answer; shown on hover with a mouse, always on touch) and Clear all at the bottom of the bell (`clearAllNotifications` → `clearAllButQuestions`: everything not cleared, older ones too, except questions still pending; asks first with the confirm, which keeps the bell open: `data-confirm` on the confirm's root, ignored by the bell's outside click). Rows fold away (`.notif-item`, 200 ms, one timer per clear) and come back where they were if saving fails. Cleared elsewhere arrives over realtime (UPDATE with `cleared_at`). Empty bell: "You're all caught up." History keeps cleared ones. (2) **Team → Defaults → Short videos**: the first row and the default people use `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(11rem / 11.5rem, 1fr))`, so a half-width card (xl: two columns) wraps instead of spilling; the skeleton matches. (3) **Contributions**: the squares don't re-fit while a popup holds the page (`scrollLocked()`; `onScrollFree()` in `use-scroll-lock.ts` measures again once it's let go); with 1.29.0's scrollbar gap this ends the grid growing under the day's list. (4) **Profile header**: @name / joined and the XP lines `text-ink-soft` (were faint), the bio `text-ink/90`. (5) **Brand**: `select-none` on the logo and the side bar's nav (a double click painted the selection colour behind "ClipTroop"). (6) **Light mode over a wallpaper**: the top bar / phone top bar `paper / 0.62` with the picture lifted (`brightness(1.3)` in the blur), a see-through side bar keeps at least 0.7 of its pane, the phone tab bar 0.72 (`html:not(.dark):has([data-dash-bg])`, globals.css). The side bar's scroll bar (`.side-scroll`): grey, only while pointing at it. Tests: `supabase/tests/notifications-clear.test.sql`, `scripts/e2e/notifications.e2e.ts` (run here against Postgres 16 + PostgREST 12.2.3, incl. a database without 0095: all checks pass).
- **1.29.0** (Oct 21 2026): **Outliers that fit your niche.** (0094; no new dependency) The owner's Outliers list of Oct 21 2026, plus charts that stop where the data does. (1) **Which channels count** (`modules/outliers/lib/fit.ts`, pure, `tests/outliers-fit.test.ts`): `channelProfile` (its titles' topic words, what it says about itself counting like a title, categories, languages incl. Hinglish via `text.ts`'s `hi` list, made-for-kids share, `regions.ts` `guessRegion`), `nicheProfile` (own channels' words, the titles its keywords found (a word only one channel uses doesn't count once there are 3), the keywords), `fitOf` (gates: kids → language → country → topic; topic = max(keywords in titles / 0.3, on-topic titles / 0.6, min(likeness / 0.35, on-topic / 0.15)); relevance = 0.6·topic + 0.4·categories·min(1, topic / 0.4), ok from 0.45; `FIT_VERSION` 2), `videoFits`, `namedBy` (a keyword that is a channel's name makes it one of the niche's own), `teamLanguage`. `lib/regions.ts` (presets US + Canada + UK + Europe, English-speaking; country and language names). (2) **Collector** (`collector.ts` rewritten around it): steps language, repair, seeds (Explore: `EXPLORE[].seeds` handles, `SEED_MIN_SUBS`), searches (only fitting videos bring their channel in; every hit stored with `ok`), channels (half the time at most; the focused niche first; own and tracked channels read deep, 150 long videos), fit (`fitNiche`: the niche's profile again every 12 h or when its key changes; a channel judged only once its uploads were read or 8 videos are stored), featured, numbers, thumbnails, alerts, audience (`buildAudience`: `analytics_breakdowns` suggested_by + `RELATED_VIDEO` for the team's 15 best videos, a note when it can't), formats / purge (Explore), insights. Explore has its own buckets (`budget.ts` `exploreUnits` / `exploreSearches`; `youtube.ts` `spender(..., {explore})`), `playlist()` pages up to 4 times, `searchChannels()`. `requests.ts`: `prepareChannel` (reads deep when asked even if read shallowly lately), `findChannels` (collected by title / handle, then YouTube by handle, a channel search only on request and logged as one of the person's searches), `addToNiche`. (3) **Actions**: `setNicheOnlyGroup`, `nicheFromChannels`, `exploreNow` (lock 240 s), `addChannelsToNiche`, `lookUpChannels`, `hideChannel` / `unhideChannel`, `loadAudience`; `setTracked` and `loadChannelDetail` read the channel right away; `createNiche` / `updateNiche` take langs, regions, onlyGroup, kids and the own channels (`syncOwnChannels`), falling back to the old columns before 0094. The page kicks a niche whose fit isn't checked yet (`after()`), widens For you's period when it's thin (says so), "taking off" = 7 days from 1.3×. (4) **UI**: `outliers-view.tsx` (gear → `settings-dialog.tsx` with Alerts and Hidden channels; the sticky bar `data-shell="subbar"` with `NichePicker` (`niche-picker.tsx`), edit, Long / Shorts and the tabs; banners for checking / filling / exploring with "Use as my niche"; "Make it ours" gone), `channels-tab.tsx` (Only videos from your channels, modes In the niche / Your channels / Found / Not a fit / Tracked / Removed with counts, `FitChip`, a Search button, other channels by that name with Add, `AddChannelsDialog` with `channel-finder.tsx`, ⋯ `ChannelMenu`, cards on phones, refreshes while channels are read), `tabs/audience-tab.tsx` (new), `niche-dialog.tsx` (channels with Only these, languages like your channel / pick / any, where creators are from, made for kids; presets add the Explore niche's channels; "Use as my niche" copies one), `idea-card.tsx` (tile: the lead outlier's thumbnail and multiplier, title, whose video, the hook, Start, Save; full view in a dialog; `leadSources` avoids repeating a picture), Ideas (Show more, a Find button for the topic, `NoIdeas` empty states), `card-menu.tsx` (hide a channel from any card), `context.tsx` (`previews` for the panel's first picture, `OutliersBlocked` with Undo), video panel (preview while loading, hide, brightness via `pct255` with help), channel panel (hide / show again, each niche's status with Add / Take out / Put back, reading banner that looks again), Search / Keywords / Title lab on a button, Saved without No folder, `bits.tsx` (bigger `Mult`, `Toggle`, `VERDICTS`, `FitChip`, icons), `filters.ts` scope `audience`, loading skeleton. `use-scroll-lock.ts` keeps the scrollbar's width as padding (`--scroll-gap`), so opening a panel or dialog no longer shifts the page. Glass CSS for the subbar (Liquid moves the top bar's fade below it). (5) **Charts** (`trend-math.ts` `lastIndex` / `reportedUntil` / `addReported`; `runningTotal` stops at the last number): the Overview's "together" line and cards' sparks stop where every platform has reported (a platform more than 4 points behind counts as stopped, not late), `LineChart` / `StackedColumns` say "No data yet" past a series' last number and draw nothing there (callers no longer turn gaps into 0), Revenue's per-day money is null after YouTube's last day, the dashboard Views widget ends its line there and compares the 7 days to that day with the 7 before ("views in the 7 days to Oct 19"). (6) Search catalog (Audience, Outlier settings, Hidden channels), /setup lists the two new env vars, privacy mentions the viewers list, the mock's thumbnail numbers are 0–255 like the database. Tests: `tests/outliers-fit.test.ts`, `supabase/tests/outliers-fit.test.sql`, `tests/trend-math.test.ts` (no data yet), `scripts/e2e/outliers.e2e.ts` (run here against Postgres 16 + PostgREST 12.2.3: all checks pass, incl. the new cooking-channel, finding, own-channels and hiding checks).
- **1.28.0** (Oct 21 2026): **Profiles with levels, Liquid glass and wallpapers.** (0093; no new dependency) The owner's list of Oct 21 2026. (1) **Glass** (`lib/look.ts`: Look {on, style simple|liquid, scope cards|everywhere, sidebar, level 0–100}, `readLook` / `cleanLookPatch` / `lookColumns` / `lookAttributes`; `lib/look-server.ts` `getLook()`; `components/ui/glass.tsx` `GlassSync` (account → device, "vp-look" in localStorage, the first-paint script in `app/layout.tsx` reads it), `LiquidLight` (pointer → `--lx` / `--ly` on `<html>`, rAF, not on touch or with motion off), `GlassSettings` (style cards, Where, Side bar, slider saved after 500 ms); `setLook()` in settings actions). Words over a wallpaper: `.wall-tone` (`--bg-luma`, `--bg-dim` → `--words-dark`, `--mismatch`; `.wall-words`, the dashboard greeting), widgets more frosted when the picture fights their words. Wallpapers measured before 1.28.0 are measured once on the dashboard (`DashboardBackdrop measure`). (3) **Profiles** (`lib/profile/progress.ts`: XP table, `levelFor` / `levelFloor` / `levelProgress` / `levelTint` (a colour per 10 levels, a hexagon from 100), `FRAMES`, `readStats`, 25 `ACHIEVEMENTS` and `achievementsFor()`; `modules/profile/layout.ts` (8 showcases: level, stats, contributions, achievements, specialties, about, teams, featured; `readProfileLayout`, `addShowcase`); `modules/profile/actions.ts` `saveProfilePage` / `setProfilePublic`; components `parts.tsx` (ProfileAvatar with its frame, LevelBadge, XpBar, AchievementBadge), `showcases.tsx`, `profile-view.tsx` (header, Customize profile: Add showcase, Frame, Background, Public / Private, Reset / Cancel / Save; `?edit=1` opens it)). `/u/[username]`: a private profile shows only the name and picture (and Invite to team for masters); the page reads `profile_pages` (RLS) and `profile_stats`. Settings → Profile: who sees your profile + Customize. Bio saved on the profile page (`updateProfile`, set-password reads it there). (4) **The dashboard board is generic**: `modules/dashboard/components/grid-board.tsx` (`GridBoard<T extends GridItem>`, `GridCardMeta`, `narrowAt`, `cq`) is the old Board + WidgetCard; the dashboard passes its CATALOG through `widgetMeta`, profiles their SHOWCASES (`prof-cq`, stacking under 760 px). Containers are named (`dash`, `prof`). (5) **Leave team** (`leaveTeam()` in team actions → `leave_team`, masters get `member_left`), Team → Team → Leave team; **Delete account** (`settings/account-actions.ts` `deleteAccount({email, password})`: the email typed, the password checked with a session-less client, no team owned or joined (service key), then the avatars and dashboard-backgrounds folders removed and `auth.admin.deleteUser`; the database cascades the rest; signs out and goes to `/login?deleted=1`), `settings/delete-account.tsx` (teams to leave first, two dialogs). (6) Dashboard fixes: the digital clock's digits capped (`DigitalClock` scale), the objectives rings' middle sized to fit (`fitRingText`, `ringHole`), resizing from the right (the grab's start box kept, `over` flag and the size badge, no easing, past the board's edge it grows left). - **1.27.5** (Oct 21 2026): **Scripts keep everything, glass, and a dashboard of your own.** (0092; no new dependency) The owner's list of Oct 20 2026, in one patch release. (1) **The formatting-loss bug**: React server actions send objects without a prototype as temporary references (`"$T"`), and ProseMirror's `attrs` are `Object.create(null)`: every save dropped every attribute (picture `src`, link `href`, heading `level`, highlight colour, list `type` / `start`, checkbox `checked`, `textAlign`). `modules/scripts/lib/paste-images.ts` `readyToSave()` always returns a plain `JSON.parse(JSON.stringify())` copy (the editor's flush and the side page's save go through it); `lib/repair.ts` `repairDoc()` on open drops pictures without an address, gives a link whose words are a web address its `href` back, and makes level-less headings Heading 2. Scripts saved before 1.27.5 lost those once (links with other words, pictures, highlights need adding again). (2) **Writing**: `lib/script-keys.ts` (`ScriptKeys`, priority 50): Tab in a list sinks the item, after "1." / "a." starts a list, else inserts a tab character (`.script-doc` `tab-size`); Shift+Tab lifts or removes it. `ScriptOrderedList` (type 1 a A i I, `data-list-type`, inline `list-style-type`; input rule `^([aAiI])\.\s$`), nesting 1. → a. → i. (`lib/list-markers.ts`, pure). Word export: `new Tab()` and one numbering reference per list with its format and start. (3) **Pictures**: `components/use-script-images.ts` (`useScriptImages`): pasted / dropped files and `data:` pictures in pasted HTML show at once (blob URL, "Uploading…") and upload to `script-images` (`<team>/<script>/<uuid>-<name>`); a save waits for uploads (`forSave` → null, retried in 700 ms) and swaps in the real addresses; `pastePlan()` decides file vs Word text + files vs a plain paste. (4) **PDF**: `lib/print-script.ts`: printing builds a plain copy (`DOMSerializer`) as `#script-print-root` straight under `<body>` with every other child of body hidden (`html.vp-printing`), waits for pictures and fonts, prints; afterprint removes it. The old `visibility: hidden` + absolute print target made Firefox print blank pages for long documents. (5) **Comments** copy with "Ready for review / staging" (`handOffScript`) and, after asking, with "Copy from…" (`copyCommentsFrom`): drawings are copied by the server (admin storage `copy`) first, then `script_copy_comments`. Clicking: a comment wins over a writing suggestion on the same word (`GrammarLayer skipInside`, the "Suggestion here" button), links have `cursor: pointer`. (6) **Long videos**: `deleteProject(id, _, { stay, confirmTitle })` (the typed name must match; removes the script pictures and sketches folders), `getVideoWork(id)` (what goes with it, who wrote in its scripts lately), `delete-video-dialog.tsx` (two steps: the summary with a warning when a script was edited in the last 15 minutes, then the name), `video-menu.tsx` on every grid card and table row (masters). Grid / table and step chips switch instantly with skeletons (`ViewToggle`, `FilterChip`, PendingNav / PendingSwap; `loading.tsx` picks the skeleton from the view cookie), shorts too. (7) **Dashboard**: resize from every edge and corner (`grid.ts` `resize` with `left` / `top`: the opposite edge stays; with the board's gravity a widget can't sit lower than the space above it allows), the Followers graph (`followersLine`, `AreaGraph` in a `GraphRoom` that draws only when tall enough; in a small box the graph wins over the platform chips, whose numbers move to the tooltip), the digital clock (`skin: "digital"`, seven segments), Top videos platform chips (saved in the widget's settings; up to 8 per platform from `loadDashAudience`), Contributions colours from every theme (`CONTRIB_THEME` = follow your own), Upcoming longs, RSVP on Next meeting (`RsvpButtons size="xs" fill`), the full-width Add a widget. Weather through `GET /api/weather` (`?q=` cities, `?lat=&lon=&units=`: two tries, 6 s timeout, Next's data cache 1 day / 15 min, coordinates rounded to ~1 km; the widget caches 6 h per place and retries once). `MonthCalendar` waits up to 1.5 s for the month's dots behind a skeleton (also before hydration) so days and dots arrive together. (8) **Best time to post** for every platform together (`allBestTimes()` in `insights-math.ts`: "all" per kind when 2+ platforms have one, each video's × typical against its own platform; `heatWaiting` = platforms still short of 12). (9) **Invite from a profile**: `/u/[username]` always shows Invite to team to a master, each team marked member / invited (`team_invites` pending and live) / available. (10) **Files after posting**: `lib/media-keep.ts` one `KEEP_CHOICES` list for shorts, long videos and Thumbnail Studio (1 day–1 year) plus never (null; `readKeep`, `keepsLess`, `KEEP_OPTIONS`); `saveKeep()` in team actions; the clean-up skips never. (11) **Glass** (`components/ui/glass.tsx`, `profiles.glass_ui`, `vp-glass`, `<html data-glass>`; CSS in `globals.css`): the wash (`body::before`: the accent and two hues beside it with CSS relative colours, meetings' violet and long videos' blue where unsupported), see-through `bg-surface` / brighter `bg-surface-2`, blur only on `[data-shell]` bars, `.dash-item` and `.glass-pane` pop-ups. (12) **Dashboard background** (`lib/dashboard-bg.ts`, `settings/dashboard-background.tsx`, `modules/dashboard/components/backdrop.tsx`): upload (photos resized to 3840 px; GIFs, animated PNG / WebP / AVIF and videos as they are), on / off, darken 0–80 %, remove; the dashboard draws it behind `[data-dash-bg]` (frosted widgets and top bar, a halo behind the greeting); videos don't play with animations off. Privacy policy: LanguageTool, Open-Meteo, GIPHY, public picture addresses. Tests: `tests/script-writing.test.ts`, `tests/dashboard-bg.test.ts`, more in `dashboard-grid`, `insights-math`, `long-review`, `public-pages`.
- **1.27.0** (Oct 20 2026): **Outliers.** (0091; new dependency `jpeg-js` 0.4.4: owner runs `npm install`) Research on public YouTube videos, no AI. (1) **Data and collector** (`modules/outliers/lib/`): `youtube.ts` (Data API with `YOUTUBE_API_KEY` or the team's channel token; search, videos, channels, playlists, captions-free), `budget.ts` (`OUTLIERS_*` env, `outlier_take`), `ingest.ts` (pure: API items → rows, kind from the channel's Shorts / long lists or length and shape, chapters, playlists, channel / video links), `collector.ts` (`runOutliersJob({team} | {explore})`: a time budget per call, steps searches (spread over the day, cached), featured / related channels, each channel's uploads (Shorts and long lists apart), stats for young videos (2 h / 12 h), details again before 30 days, thumbnails (`thumb-math.ts`: jpeg-js decode → main colours, brightness, colourfulness, a 64-bit look-alike hash), scores (`scores.ts`: views ÷ the median of the channel's 30 previous videos of the same kind; `projected` from `shareAt()`), relevance of each channel to the niche, formats (`formats.ts`, 49 formats, `FORMAT_VERSION`), insights (`insights.ts` `buildInsights`: per kind formats, topics, keywords, templates, title model, numbers, colours, lengths, posting times; `all` only when a kind has under 40 videos), alerts (`outliers_alert_candidates`: a tracked channel's or a watched word's video heading past `alert_min`; the 4 strongest each, the rest in one) and the Monday summary (`outlier_digest`), `outliers_purge`. (2) **The engine** (pure, browser and server): `text.ts` (tokens incl. "4s", "5G", hyphenated words; glue and style words; topic words), `keywords.ts` (names; a kind of a name before it ("fake iPhone", also of a model's family) and the kind of video after it ("GoPro review", whole: "… tier list"); never a cut name, verbs or glue; swallowing into longer names; `place`, `shape`, `stock` flags; `suggestKeywords` for a first niche), `skeleton.ts` (title templates with {X} {$} {N} {T} {Y}; model names and suffixes stay in {X}), `titles.ts` (openers, closers, traits, words, lengths, `scoreTitle` 1–99 with what helps and holds it back), `ideas.ts` (`generateIdeas`: a winning format × a strong or rising topic, the format's patterns or real templates, plural-aware, seeded by day, no format takes over, too close to the team's own videos left out; `ideasFromVideo`, `rewrites`), `similar.ts` (titles and thumbnails alike), `filters.ts` (URL ↔ filters ↔ the database's jsonb). (3) **The page** `/outliers` (`app/(dashboard)/outliers/page.tsx` loads only the open tab; `lib/view.ts`; tabs For you, Search, Ideas, Formats, Keywords, Channels, Title lab, Thumbnails, Saved; long videos | Shorts; every niche / one / Explore; the video panel `?v=` and channel panel `?c=` over any tab with their own history entries; `?open=niche|alerts`; a setup card while the team has no niche; collecting banner that refreshes by itself; the access banner). Components in `modules/outliers/components/` (context with three contexts so a save doesn't redraw everything, bits, video cards / rows / table, charts, panels, save menu with folders, idea cards (Start a video / Short → `startFromIdea` → `/videos/new?idea=` or `/shorts/new?idea=`, filled by `lib/idea-prefill.ts`; only masters and schedulers, others are told to save it), niche and alerts dialogs, tabs). Tabs other than For you, the panels and the dialogs load in their own chunks (preloaded on hover / tap, panels and dialogs while idle). Thumbnails show their main colour while loading. - **1.25.1** (Oct 15 2026): **0089 installs on Supabase.** The first push stopped at statement 82 (`create or replace function global_search … set pg_trgm.word_similarity_threshold = 0.45`): "permission denied to set parameter" (42501). Supabase runs migrations as `postgres`, which isn't a superuser; until pg_trgm's library is loaded in the session, Postgres doesn't know that setting and treats it as superuser-only (0024 and 0026 loaded it by building trigram indexes first; CI runs as a superuser, so it passed). Fix: a DO block before the function calls `<pg_trgm schema>.similarity('a', 'a')`. Checked as a role that isn't a superuser in a fresh session: refused without it, accepted with it. `scripts/db/test-from-scratch.sh` now refuses a migration from 0089 on that has `set pg_trgm.` before a `.similarity(` call (docs/DEVELOPMENT.md, Branches, CI and migrations). No app code changed. 0089 never got recorded as run, and it's safe to run twice, so the Database Action simply runs it again.
